1⃣&2⃣

"A guje ta fado gidan dauke da trayn ta na talla tana fadowa tayi wurgi dashi,


Tuni mahaifiyar ta dake tsugune tana fifita wuta ta Mara mata baya, 

Saida ta mayarda kofar ta g'ark'ame kafin ta dafe kirjin ta tana mayar da numfashi,


Cikin haki ta kalle ta " kekuwa  Haneefa  me ya koroki yau wa kika tono mana?"


Ta fada cike da tausayi daga ganin Alama sun saba wasan tseren'


Bajewa tayi tareda tuntsirewa da dariya  "kai ummi  daga ganin na shigo sai ki biyo ni Wallahi kin cika tsoro' ta kuma tuntsirewa da wata dariyar,


" yo ba dole ba kullum sai kin takalo mana magana jiya tala har mari na tayi cikin gidan nan saboda fitinar ki'



"To ba  d'an ta yaja ba' kuma kullum sai surika kirana da shegiya ''wai da gaske ummi ni shegiya ce?


" bata fuska tayi tareda tsaki ta bude dakin ta fito tana mai cewa "na fada miki ki daina  biyewa zancen jama'a ke ma ya'ce kamar ko wa,

" eh ummi na' to ki maida hankalin ki akan karatun ki ni nayi Alkawarin kulawa da ilimin ki dai dai gwargwado da sana'ar da mukeyi ta rufin Asiri insha Allahu zan cika miki burin ki'"



"Rungume ta tayi " na daina tanbayar ki ummi tunda bakyaso  ta furta cike da damuwa ta kwanto daurin kudin da ta yo cinikin  gyadar ta ta mika mata "gashi na siyar duka"



Ta juya "zan shirya lokacin islamiyya yayi ta wuce dakin nasu ciki daya tal,

Cike take da zumudi a lokacin da take cikin motar zuwa Niger ita da Ummin ta gaba daya farin ciki ne fal a zuciyar ta sai dai a gefen ummi Abun ba haka yake ba domin gaba daya hankalin ta a tashe yake,

Ita dai kallon gari kawai takeyi bata taba shigowa garin minna ba sai wannan karon shiyasa take ta baza ido ba Abinda yafi burgeta a garin na Niger irin roundabout din da aka kawata da Abubuwa kala kala na ban sha'awa

*YA'YAN GATA*



_written by Fenerh_


_Dedicated to_
Real smersher


1⃣3⃣&1⃣4⃣

Ido Haneefa ta zuba ma Ummi kafin ta kalli Ma'ma"Ban fahimta ba ? Waye ya haife ni ummi?


Ta matsa gab da ita Ummi ban gane me Ma'ma ke nufi ba?


"Itama  Samra Abin ya daure mata kai " momy ce dake tsaye tayi saurin matsawa "Haneefa wannan maganar manya ce ki kiyi shiru haka'.


" momy kinaji fa me Ma'ma ta fada "tace ita ce mama na ba ummi ba " ni kufitar dani a duhu na gaji haka nan"


Ta fada tareda sanya kuka mai ban tausayi "ita kanta momy tasan Yarinyar na cikin sarka kiya sosai,


" Dady ne ya kalli Ma'ma "Saratu kin kyauta kinji " ya fada cikin fushi "fitina kullum kike burin haddasa wa saboda tuna nin ki na banza to kinyi nasara ki sa ruwa akasa ka sha mafarkin ki ya zama Gaskiya zan Auri Asiya".



" wata irin kururuwa ta saka "Wallahi Aliyu kayi kadan baka isa ba " saidai in bana numfashi Amma baza ka taba Auren wannan karuwar ba,


"Saurin matsawa yayi zai watsa mata mari  Momy tayi saurin tarewa " Ashsha Alhaji Aliyu ya zaka biyewa mace kazo kana daka sani adaiyi hakuri ku fahimci juna nidai a tunani na Asiya batada matsala ko kadan idan har zakuyi mata Adalci'"



"Aa Hajiya Sadiya  ya zaki nuna min sonkai aa to da,sake na d'auka zaki goyi bayana sai kije kina goyon bayan wannan matsiya ciyar talaka,


"Ki barta Hajiya Allah ne kawai zai hana ni Auren Asiya domin ina matukar kaunar ta, matsawa yayi  kusa da Ummi,


" Asiya ki rufa min Asiri ki Aure ni Jan ran ya isa haka shekara nawa kina gara Kaina na wahalu haka zakiyi ta zama bakiyi Aure ba A rayuwar ki bayan kinsan nine nafi dacewa da ke,


"Wani kallo Haneefa ke binsu da shi " me kuma hakan ke nufi?
"Ummi bata taba Aure ba kenan " wai ita ya ma Akayi aka haife ta ne?

"Kodai zancen mutane Gaskiya ne ita shegiya ce ko kuma da gasken ba Ummi ta haife ta ba,


Saurin girgiza kai tayi ta riko momy " Don Allah ki ce su fadamin Abinda yake faruwa wallahi sun daure min kai zuciya ta zata iya bugawa,

Ta saki momy ta nufi Ummi "ki fadamin wacece mahaifiya ta?
" hawayen tausayin Kansu ummi ta share "Haneefa kina kokon to ne?

Nice nan mahaifiyar ki kuma ga baban ki nan" kuka tasa "kenan ni shegiyar ce? Ta fada tana Jan baya tareda girgiza kai,



Saurin matsawa Dady yayo kusa da ita ya rungume ta " ke ba shegiya ba Haneefa ke ya'ta ce ke y'ar gata ce muna matukar kaunar ki kinji,



"To Dady ku yimin bayani mana insan matsa yi na kai na na cikin duhu,
Rikota yayi ya zaunar da ita kafin ya kalle su duka'


" ku bamu wuri zanyi magana da ya'ta' "aa Aliyu Ummi ta fada cikin tsoro ka bari infada mata da Kaina ni nafi dacewa da hakan,


" ma'ma ta yi karab wallahi sai nima anyi a gaba na bazai yuwu ba,

"Momy ta ce 'Samra muje gida toh mu barsu suyi magana a tsakanin su " aa Hajiya Sadiya ai na fada miki komai don haka ki tsaya ba damuwa 



"Natsuwa sukayi dukansu kowa ya samu wuri ya zauna,
Ummi ce ta matsa kusa da Haneefa tareda riko hannun ta,



*TUNA BAYA*


" Tafiya mukeyi mu biyu nida yaya ta larai zamuje makaranta A lokacin muna mataki na karshe, "muka hadu da  wani saurayi  w'anda ya sanya min ido tun daga nesa yana kallo na,


Har muka wuce shi idanun shi na Kaina " mtsww" nayi tsaki, ya larai tace "ke kuwa lafiya?

" bakiga wani d'an sa idoba a can baya ? Tayi dariya "toh ai kullum wurin zaman shi kenan idan zamu wuce " k'arki ce min baki taba ganin shi ba?


"Toh ni ina ruwa na dashi, haka muka isa makarantar inda na hadu da Aminiya ta Saratu  na ganta sukuku ba kuzari kasan cewar ta mai son hayaniya da yawa Amma ranar na ganta shiru,


" larai ta wuce Abinta ta bar mu domin bata cika zama cikin mu ba,
"Sara lafiya?
" hawaye ta share "kafin ta kalle ni,


" Asiya na hadu da damu wa son maso wani '
"Aliyu da nake fada miki ina sonshi 
Yau na fada mai " Amma ya bude baki yaci mutunci na sannan kuma ya mun kashedi akan cewar yanada wadda yake so infita hanyar shi"


"Wace yarinya ce a garin nan ? Waye ubanta da Aliyu zai wula kanta ni saboda ita " wallahi idan har ina son Abu sai na same shi idan har kudi na da rana,



Kallon ta nayi cikin mamaki'
"Sara yanzu akan da' namiji kike wannan hakilon ?
" harara ta tayi "eh akan Aliyu zan iya komai don haka idan Bakida Shawara ki kyale ni,


Sanin halin ta na bar ta na wuce Abuna,
na manta da maganar ta gaba daya,


" ranar ina zaune a cikin gida muna taya Gwaggo n mu aiki yaro yayi sallama,


"Wai ana kiran Asiya"
"Nayi karab nace kace bata nan'
" larai ta kalle ni "ki wuce ko infada wa Gwaggo"

"Banida zabi dole na fita " wannan saurayin ne da muka hadu dashi kwanan baya tsaye a jikin mashin dinshi sabo dal,


Yana zuba min murmushi na d'an hade rai kafin na karaso kusa da shi'
"Malam lafiya?


" yayi dariya "Malama ita takawo haka " ya kamata a min masauki kafin tanbaya ko?

"Sanyi jikina yayi ganin baida Alamar fitina na ce ya karaso cikin zauren mu " tabar ma na dakko mai ya zauna,


"Malama Asiya na dade ina ganin ki kuma na binciki halayen ki duka nasan komai akan ki 'nidai bada wasa nazo ba zancen Aure ya kawoni,



" nayi Saurin kallon shi "Saurayine mai kwarjini " naso ince ya mun hakuri ni bazan yi Aure yanzu ba Amma na kasa ,

"Ina sauraren ki " idan kin Amince inturo iyaye na ayi magana, 
"Aa ka bari inyi Shawara kafin iyaye su shiga,


" toh ba damuwa zandawo gobe,
"Ya mike ya tafi 
Ina shigowa Gwaggo n mu ta kirani ts tanbaye ni waye?

Anan nayi mata bayani ta fara yimin nasiha,
" ki tsayar da mijin Aure Asiya ayi tareda y'ar uwarki kinga ita tanada tsayayye ke kuma fa "halin Rayuwa gani banida isashshiyar lafiya idan nagan ku a dakin ku koda na mutu hankali na zai kwanta domin kuna dakin mazajen ku"


"Aa Gwaggo kidaina mana fatan mutuwa kina nan har kika jikokin ki' " kayya kedai ki rike nasiha ta jeki,


Kamar da wasa muka kulla soyayya mai tsafta nida Aliyu har gidan su aka San da ni nima Gwaggo tayi na am dashi, kwatsam yazo min da wata magana,



A wurin Aikin shi ogan shi ya bashi mata yace ya Aura shi baya so Amma iyayen shi sun shiga ciki,

Hankali na ya tashi bayan "sun San da maganar mu shine suke nemomai wata,?

" ya fara kwantar min da hankali " Asiya k'arki damu ni kece zabin zuciya ta ke nakeso don haka koda sun dage sai na Aure ta ni kuma sai sun Aura min ke,


"Nayi matukar kokari ganin ban nuna damuwa ta ba har ya tafi na koma gida na fadawa Gwaggo " nasiha ta fara yimin,


"Ki y'arda da kaddara Asiya idan har mijin ki ne komai daren dadewa zakuyi Aure idan kuwa bakuda rabon Aure  koda duk Niger state zasu hadu bazai Aure kiba,



" nasihohi masu shiga jiki Gwaggo tayi ta mun har naji ba Abinda ke damu na,


"Wasa wasa har aka tsayar da maganar Auren shi 
nidai na barwa Allah komai kuma shi ma bai fasa bibiya ta ba


" Saratu ce tazo min da katin Auren ta cikin farin ciki "Asiya Ansa ranar Aurena da Masoyi na,
" na tayata murna ta bani labari yadda Al amarin ya faru,

Atake na gano cewa Aliyu na ne Saratu keson kuma itace baban ta ya manna mai domin yana aiki karkashin shi,


"Tuni na kara sanya wa zuciya ta hakuri domin koda Aliyu ya ce zai Aure ni bayan saratu bazan y'ar da ba don nafi kowa sanin halin ta ciki da waje,


" A ranar yazo gidan mu shima da zancen Auren shi ranshi a bace "wai har sunsa rana nan da sati uku batareda sun karbi bukatar shi ba,



" kallon shi nayi "Aliyu" 
Ya kalle ni shima "Kayimin wani Alkawari idan har kana kauna ta tsakani da Allah,


" Saurin cewa yayi "wallahi ina kaunar ki Asiya nayi miki kowane irin Alkawari ne kifada kawai,


" ka Auri saratu ka sota da zuciya daya kamar yadda kake sona ka rike ta Amana karka taba nuna mata kataba soyayya dani don Allah,


Kuma kayi hakuri domin bazan hada miji da Aminiyata ba kaje Allah ya hada kowa da rabon sa na shige gidan mu,


"Na dauki kaddara ta nayi hakuri tuni nabarwa Allah dukkan Al amurana 
Akayi Auren larai  ta tare ni kadai na rage gida ina ci gaba da karatuna har na kammala,



" ban kuma jin labarin Aliyu ba Amma nasan sunyi Aure da Saratu bayan shekara daya da gama karatuna kuma da Auren larai Gwaggo n mu ta rasu,


"Na zama marainiya gaba da baya dagani sai y'ar uwata ita kuma tana gidan mijin ta saidai inje in duba ta indawo,


Larai tayi matukar damuwa da zamana haka ta nemi da indawo gidan ta da zama saidai atafau mijinta yaki Amince mata w'anda har ya janyo musu matsala 



" ina zaune da dad'i ba dad'i nikadai budurwa wata rana saiga Saratu wadda Auren su kusan shekara daya da kusan rabi batareda ta neme ni ba,



"Nayi matukar mamakin ganin canjawar ta domin ta zama babbar mace ta zauna tana yatsina fuska akan kujerar da na bata,


" Asiya nazo intaimake ki ne naga kina cikin damuwa'
Kallon ta nayi domin koda Gwaggo ta rasu Aliyu yazo min gaisuwa Amma ita ko leke,


"Saratu kenan ni ina neman taimako na a wurin Allah ne ba mutum ba " toh naji so nake don Allah Kizo muje gidana ki tayani zama Aliyu na ya tafi south Africa zaiyi couse na kusan shekara,


"Na nuna mata nidai ta barni cikin gidan mu " muna cikin maganar ya larai tazo domin kawomin kayan Abincin da itace karfin cina yanzu,



"Ita tasa baki " Asiya ki bita hankali na ba kwance yake ba kullum idan natuna ke kadai ke cikin gidan nan gashi mijina ya hana indauke ki ,


Kije gidan Saratun zaki samu saukin kadaicin,
Da taimakon larai Saratu tasamu na Amince mata muka koma katon gidan su a lokacin duk da baikai w'anda suke ciki ba yanzu'


Mum zauna cikin jin dad'i domin a kullum cikin tarai rayata take komai nakeso shi zanyi inci maikayau insha mai kyau suturu masu kyau da kuma tsada,

"Tuni na murmure  larai ma hankalin ta ya kwanta a haka mukayi wata shida a tare 

Kwatsam ranar Aliyu ya diro Nigeria ya matukar canjawa saboda hutu da kudi " yayi mamakin gani na a gidan ni kuma banji dad'i ba domin naso kafin ya dawo inbar gidan,



Naso tafiya ta hanani wai zai koma ne ai yazo pass ne kawai na kwana biyu,


Tun dawowar shi na boye Kaina bana y'arda mu hadu dashi sam'



"Har ya koma ranar muna zaune da saratu take fadamin zan rakata checkup India saboda rashin haihuwar ta,

Naso turjiya amma nacin saratu yasa na bita,
Ashe Abinda bansani ba tasan matsalar ta ta rashin mahaifa mai kyau sanadin hakan aka fada mata bazata taba samun haihuwa ba w'anda shi kanshi Aliyun bai sani ba


" Mun shirya zuwa India w'anda ko a mafarkina ban taba tunanin zuwa ba sai gani a India w'anda bansan da wata manufa Saratu ta kaini ba,


Abinda ya ban mamaki shine bayan munje da kwana biyu Aliyu ma ya iso kasar "


Ina nan nidai nawa ido kawai saratu tazomin da zancen zan rakata Asibiti,
Bankawo komai ba mukaje tareda ita har ma Aliyu w'anda gaisuwa kawai ke shiga tsakani na dashi koda zaiyi magana bana tanka shi,



Muna zaune a reception din Asibitin mu duka ta,kawo mana drinks "na karba na shanye shima haka domin garin Akwai zafi sosai inajin kishi,


Banfarka ba sai gani na nayi akwance asaman gadon marasa lafiya " Sanye da kayan da suke sanyawa majinyata,

Nayi saurin zabura na zubawa drip din dake daure a hannu na ido "lafiya meyasa meni?


Saratu ce ta shigo da sauri ta rikeni " Bi a hankali Asiya k'arki juwa kanki bakyajin dad'i ne kuma kirika lallaba jikin ki kinji?


"Abin ya daure min kai gashi inajin jikina ba dai dai ba sannan kuma ga magan ganun ta bayan na shigo Asibitin da kafafu na,


" na kalle ta Saratu me ya sameni? Kimun bayani meke faruwa ? Bata ce komai ba ta ce min tana zuwa ta fita na bita da kallo,


Dakin da Aliyu ke kwance ta koma w'anda shima ya tashi zaune yana tanbayar lafiya?
Zama tayi gab dashi 

My D "ka kwantar da hankalin ka farin ciki nake nema mana'
Kamar ya me  ke faruwa saratu?



A nan ta mai bayani " cewar likita ya dubata a Nigeria ya kuma fada mata bazata iya haihuwa ba saboda mahaifar ta Sam batada karfin daukar ciki,


Anan yabata shawarr ta nemi mijinta inyaso shi zai taimake ta tasamu haihuwa,

Ta hanyar yiwa ubangiji shishshigi ita kuwa tuni ta Amince tareda daukar Al washin ko ta halin kaka sai ta samu kwanta a duniya ta yadda zata samu magaji ita ma domin iyayen ta masu matukar Arziki ne ga kuma Aliyu ma ya tashi yanzu,


Don haka dole ta haifi d'an kanta,

"A haka har zuciyar ta ta Amince tareda bata mummunar huduba ta hanyar cutar da marainiyar Allah,


Tana gama fada mai ya diro daga kan gadon jikin shi na rawa " Saratu kina tsoron Allah kuwa? Tayaya zaki cutar da Asiya bayan bata nufin ki da sharri "Saratu Bakida imani kinason kanki da yawa



Yarinyar da bata taba Aure ba wadda inada yaki nin budurwa ce zaka cutar da ita ta hanyar sanya mata kwai a Gaskiya sai kinbiya wannan bashin cutar war saratu,



Fita yayi ya nufi likita domin tanbayar shi ko za a iya cire ma Asiya Kwan da suka dasa mata ?
Yace bazai yuwuba zata iya samun matsala,



A haka suka sallame mu muka koma masaukin mu batareda nasan meke faruwa dani ba Har na daina jin sauyin da nakeji a jikina,



Wata daya cur muka kwashe a India Wanda munyi shine ba cikin jin dad'i ba domin Aliyu tafiyar shi yayi ya barmu na lura fada sukeyi da Saratu tun Fitowar mu Asibiti,


Mundawo Nigeria da wata daya na tsinci Kaina cikin hali na kwadayi w'anda banda shi A Rayuwa ta Sam,


Komai dare zan nemi cin nama ko kuma wani Abu daban w'anda na lura Saratu gaba daya ta maida hankalin ta Kaina,


Komai nakeso yanzu zata bani,
Har na cika wata uku da ciki a jikina batareda na sani ba, kuma bankawo komai ba domin ina Al adata kamar koda yaushe,


A lokacin ne Aliyu ya kammala couse dinshi ya dawo gida gaba daya tareda ci gaba sosai da kuma din bin Arzikin shi da ya kara bunkasa,


A lokacin ba laifi na saki jiki dashi w'anda bansan dalilin shiba haka kawai nakeson zama kusa dashi don insha ki kamshin turaren shi,


"A wannan lokacin shima wata irin kulawa da tattali yake bani komai biyu yake siyo mana da saratu 

Har kukan fita shakata wa a tare wani lokaci 
Mun dauki Hoton nan dake dakina a wata ranar da bazan taba mantawa da ita ba,



Lokacin Cikina nada wata hudu ya d'an yi girma har nafara tunanin hutune yasanya ni yin tunbi kawai sai na farajin motsi a cikin nawa,


Tun ina daukar Abin wasa har na fadawa saratu " nifa Cikina motsi nakeji ko dai banda lafiya ne?


"Batareda ta shirya ba ta ce  yarona lafiyar shi kalau kenan 
Asiya nakusa zama uwa don Allah ki kula min da cikin nan da kyau shine Rayuwa ta,


Zuba mata ido nayi cike da Alamar tanbaya?  A nan batareda shakka ba ta fadamin Akwai Ajiyar cikinta a marata,.



Nayi matukar tsorata nayi kuka nayi mata Allah ya isa yafi cikin masaki 
Amma daga baya soyayya irin ta uwa nasamu Kaina da son cikin ko banza inada Abokin Shawara duk da ba kwai na bane Amma nasan Abinda zan haida dole zaimun soyayya irin ta uwa,.



Y'ar uwata da ta samu labari tazo wurina tayi kuka sosai akan saratu ta cutar mata da kanwa w'anda har ta dauke ni ta tafi dani a cewar ta ko Auren ra zai mutu bazata kuma barina ba,


Hakan kuwa ya faru domin sanadi na Auren larai ya samu matsala w'anda suka rabu da mijinta " ya larai ta saida d'an gidan Gwaggo n mu da muke ciki ta siya mana a wata unguwa wato tunga domin Nisan tani da Saratu,


Duk da haka tana nan biye damu domin Aliyu ma a lokacin ya kwallafa rai akan cikin jikina mu samman ganin irin girman da yakeyi,



"Cikina na tsufa hankali na na kara tashi domin tsoron ina haihuwa za araba ni da shi 
Nan da nan na fara rama sai ciki tirtsitsi 


Ganin hankali na yaki kwanciya ne yasa larai bani Shawara in tattara inkoma kauyen su Gwaggo n mu 


Kasan cewar inada kudi sosai a atareda ni yasa bamu sha wahala ba muka koma lafai,


Saratu hauka ne kawai batayi ba lokacin da Abin ya faru w'anda shi kanshi Aliyu hankalin shi ya tashi sosai saidai ba adauki lokaci ba Saratu ta kawomai Hanan da sunan zata riketa tayi Adopting dinta daga gidan marayu,



Kuka Haneefa tasa domin dai ita yanzu batasan matsayin taba uba daya uwa biyu kenan kuma kowa nada hakki akanta domin tanada ilimi dai dai gwar gwado


Saurin matsowa Ummi tayi ta rungume ta 
" ki yafeni Haneefa bansan y'anda zanyimiki bayani bane tunfar ko domin nasan bazaki fahimce niba Amma yau kinji ke wacece 

Ni na dauki cikin ki wata tara sannan saratu itace ainihin mahaifiyar ki'
Kuka ta kuma sawa "aa Ummi ni kece uwata bakya mugunta bakyason kanki


Amma Ma'ma tana son kanta kuma ina sonki fiye da komai Ummi na " sannan meyasa baki fadamin inna larai yayarki bace?


Girgiza kai tayi "Haneefa fada miki na dai dai da tona Asirin da nake boye miki ne domin zakiso kiji mene ne dalilin barinta da nayi shiyasa na fada miki kawata ce 


Eh larai ita kadai nakeda itace matsayin gurbin uwa a gareni ta zamar min babban bangon da nake dafawa in mike inaso kiyimin hakuri zanci cigaba da baki sauran labari na nan gaba Amma yanzu kinji ke wa cece"


"Dady ne ya matso kusa da ita " nagode wa Allah Asiya da kika  fahimci cewar banida masa niya akan komai,


"Girgiza kafa ta farayi ta kalli Haneefa" zo nan my daughter yanzu kinsan cewa ke jinin Arziki ce bata tsiya ba ko"?

"Aa Ma'ma kibarni da Ummi na nafi sonta kuma nayi miki Alkawarin yimiki biyayya irin na uwa Amma kibarni da Ummi na nafi sabawa da ita,


" shiru tayi kafin tace" na Amince Amma da sharad'in zaki rika zuwa gidana kina kwana wani lokaci,


"Na y'ar da ta fada tareda rungume Ummin ta " Samra na tsaye tana mai jin tausayin kawar tata 

Duk jikin su yayi sanyi 
Ta matsa jikin Inna larai ta rungume ta "ina sonki Inna larai saboda ke jinin  Ummi na ce kuma kin sa daukar da komai saboda farin cikin Ummi na Allah yasaka miki da mafificin Aljanna,

Ta girgiza kai " kidaina fadar haka Haneefa ina son Asiya sosai ina son kwanciyar hankalin ta domin ita kadai nake gani inji sanyi,


"Kuma bazan taba yin Abinda zai cutar da itaba komai na aika ta a Rayuwa ta inada dalili " sannan ina rokon ku da ku gafar ce ni a duk lokacin da kuka sameni da wani KUSKUREN,

"Karkice haka yaya larai baki taba mun laifi ba tsawon Rayuwa ta " aa Asiya d'an Adam ajizini 

Toh nidai na yafe miki koda kinmin laifi yaya
Haka suka watse ma'ma da Dady suka tafi gidansu Samra ma haka ita da Momy suka barsu a cikin sabon gidan su,


A kwance take a tafkeken dakin nata da yamafi na Hanan haduwa tana tunanin rayuwar ta yau itace a cikin daula Allah mai yadda yaso,

A nan ta tuna da takardar da ta tsinta a dakin Samra mai dauke da furanni masu birgewa sakone a takaice da rubutu mai kyau,


Three things cannot be long hidden …… The sun, The moon and The truth.


Jujjuya takardar tayi tareda jefar da ita domin bata fahimci komai ba,

Lafiyayyar walima Ma'ma ta fara kokarin shirya wa domin nunawa duniya jinin ta gagarumin fati ne take shirya wa tuni ta yada shi a k'afofin watsa labarai 


"Har su Facebook hotunan su ne ake watsa wa ya'yan gata untouchable party w'anda aka hada ita da Hanan,


Duk wannan budurin Hanan ta boye kanta ta zuba musu ido har Ma'ma ta kawo musu tsadaddun outfit kusan kala biyar biyar ita da Haneefa,

Ummi nata ido Dady dai ya bada gudun muwa domin shima yanason asan da zaman ya'yan shi, 


Domin yana yiwa Hanan so na tsakani da Allah har cikin zuciyar shi bai Rabata da Haneefa ba ,

Akwance yake a gadon dake cikin masaukin shi dawowar shi kenan daga wasa ya zubawa saman dakin ido 


" dole inbar garin nan kafin hawan jini ya kamani I want to see her, ya fada a fili tareda kifewa "I love you Haneefa" ya furta Kamar tana gaban shi,

Laptop dinshi ya janyo domin duba email dinshi sakonni ne Kala kala saidai Hoton sune ya dauki hankalin shi yayi saurin zabura ya tashi zaune,


Kyakkyawar fuskar ta ce akan idon shi ya shafo ta kamar ya janyota fili yakeji tayi matukar yin kyau a cikin Arabian gown lemon green da mayafinta kana ganin ta kasan tana hutawa yanzu'



"Bata fuska yayi bayan ya yi serving Hoton " ji yadda kowa ke ganin hotunan ta a duniya,
Wani kishine kuma ya rufe shi nan da nan Gaskiya an cuce shi da za a kama yada mai Hoton mata a duniya........✍

*YA'YAN GATA*



_written by Fenerh_


_Dedicated to_
Real Smersher


*kedin ta daban ce my smersher domin gada daya littafin na sadaukar da shine a gareki domin kin cancanci hakan ke Alkhairi ce Allah kuma ya karemin ke daga dukkan sharri,*

*Masoya masu yimin fatan Alkhairi ina biye da ku duka ina kuma matukar godiya Allah ya saka muku da khairan*


1⃣5⃣&1⃣6⃣
A kwance take tana k'arkad'a kafa kaya ne Ma'ma ta shigo dasu  kusan kala goma

A bayan ta wata mata ce  d'auke da d'an karamin kit na kayan kwalliya 

"Ya kina kwance har yanzu baki shirya ba ga y'ar uwarki can har ta fito ita, gashi jama'a sun taru har su Dadyn ku ma suna wajen ke ko wanka Bakida niyyar yi?


Ko tak bata cewa Ma'ma ba saida ta kuma mai maitawa kafin ta bude baki da kyar'
" toh shi kenan kuje Kuyi murnar ganin y'ar ku ni dama ba dole sai naje ba,


"Baki Ma'ma ta saki tana mamakin fitsarar Hanan yanzu' kafin cikin fushi ta fara fad'a , " kada Allah yasa ki fito kinji ki kwanta Yarinyar kawai da bakisan mutunci ba'


Inkin so kita zama cikin kuncin rai ruwanki. Ta buga tsaki ta fita tana ta ruwan bala'i,

"Matar tace ki tashi ki shirya kinji mutane sun gama taruwa " tsawa ta buga mata "ki fita min a daki ko dole ne zuwa partyn nace bazan jeba,dole ta fita ta kyaleta,



"Harabar gidan ta cika makil da mutane Samari ne masu ji da Kansu ya'yan wasu kuma a gwamnati,



Su Dady na gefe daya shida Abokan shi suna tattaunawa yana cikin Adon manyan kaya na burgewa 
Daka ganshi kasan yana cikin nishad'i,


********
Masha Allah shine kawai na furta domin shigar da tayi cikin wata doguwar riga kamar  wedding gown  pink colour mai dogon hannu sai gyale golden da kuma takalmin ta,ma har Po's dinta kalar golden tayi matukar haduwa  


Gefen ta Inna larai ce da kuma Ummi suma cikin Adon burgewa Abinsu  
Suka nufo wurin taron " gaba daya idon jama'ar akan su ya sauka,


"Kowa na fadin tubarkalla masha Allah da irin wannan halittar domin Haneefa karshe ce wurin kyau da kuma kyan diri " gayu tuni sun rude,


Dady bakin shi har kunne yana kallon Ummi da kuma gudan jinin shi Haneefa cikin tsana nin farin ciki,


"Ummi ta nufi wurin su momy da ta hango su a zaune dasu Samra har ma da Ibrahim da little Dady Inna larai ma ta bita domin gujewa fitinar saratu,


" Ma'ma ce ta gabatar da ita wurin kawayen ta y'an gayu irinta kowa na masha Allah har daya daga cikin su ta ce"Hajiya Saratu nidai nayi wa fahad kamu '


Tayi Dariya cike da iyayi "ai kuwa sun dace 'ina ma yake fahad din?
" ai tare mukazo bari inkirashi bansan inda ya shiga ba,.


"Daya daga ciki ta ce" Gaskiya kinyimin shigar sauri Hajiya Hasana  da na kamawa Ahmed dina, 



Wani kyakkyawan gaye ne ba laifi ya iso wurin su tareda cewa gani Momy,

Na kiraka ne dama kaga y'ar uwarka' Idanun shi akanta daman tun Fitowar ta yaji ya Afka tuni zuciyar shi ta yaba da ita,


"Itadai kawai tana kallon su ne domin wayewar su tayi yawa Sam basu wani burgeta ba ita tafi son Ummin ta komai simple a Rayuwa ba wani Alfahari,



"Dady ne ya matso batareda ya damu da gayyar kawayen momy ba ya riko hannun ta " muje Haneefa kiga Abokai na,


Cikin ladabi ta gaishe su sun kuma yaba sosai da irin tarbiyar Asiya,


Samra ta nufota tareda rungume ta " suka nufi wasu kujeru a can gefe suna tattauna matsalar dake tsakanin ta da Hanan,



A tsaye take ta Saman dakinta tana hango duk Abinda ke wakana a harabar gidan,


"Tsayuwar motar ce tasa gaba daya hankulan mutane ya koma wurin a ciki kuwa harda su Haneefa,

Motace kalar ja mai duhu mulmulalliya Saman ta a bude " saidai ba motar kawai bace abin kallo w'anda ke cikin motar ne yafi daukar hankalin mutane,



"Fitowa yayi tareda rufe saman motar bayan ya danna key din hannun shi 
Y'an mata  tuni sun rude domin hango gaye mai tashen suna a duniyar super eagle da kuma tashin kudi ga kyau da kuma kwarjini,



" matasa kuwa sun fara raina wankan su domin ganin ogan su a Ado, 
Sumar nan da aka ajiye irin ta y'an kwallo abin burgewa ,


"Momy da Ibrahim suka kalli juna cike da mamaki'
" yaushe yaron nan ya shigo gari?
Little Dady ne ya ruga da gudu ya Dane shi cike da murna,


"Tuni yan mata aka fara gyara dressing domin ya kya'sa shikuwa ko wadda yazo domin ta bai kalla ba,



"Momy na ba kiyi murnar gani na bane? " aa mamaki nakeyi kawai,
Ya rungume yayan shi brother, 


"Kai ko ka riga ka saba zuwan bazata ko? Wai ma me yashigo dakai bayan gobe kunada wasa?



" dole ce ta kawoni. dariya yayi kawai domin yasan halin kanin nashi 
"Ya gaida su Ummi cikin girma kafin ya riko hannun Little Dady " Ina Auntyn ka?


"Aunty Samra taje yawo AUNCLE" to ga yarinya ta can tareda Haneefa magulmaci,


"Nop Hanan nake nema' 
" gani nan' ta fada tana karaso wa cikin shiga irin ta Haneefa,
Domin tana hango Shigowar shi ta shirya cikin sauri tareda sanya nata kayan ta fito cikin Adon ta na burgewa,




"Mamaki Ma'ma takeyi domin ta San har dady ya mata magana tace ita bata ra ayin zuwa partyn sai gashi yanzu ta sakko da kanta, to komene ne dalili?



" Momy dai dariya tayi tareda cewa "to mu ku bamu wuri zamu yi magana,
" ni dai momy zan tafi daman inada uzuri "a sauka lafiya Amma ka ga Haneefa kafin ka wuce ko'



" toh yace kawai domin yasan manufar momy a tsakanin shi da Haneefa, 
Shi kanshi yasan yana son ta saidai soyayyar da d'an uwan shi ke mata tafi tashi dan haka dole ya hakura ya barwa zuciyar shi,



Hanan ce ta bishi saida sukaje gab da su Samra yaja musu kujera "Hanan mu zauna a nan ko? 

Sai a lokacin Samra taga yayan ta, ihun murna tasa tareda tashi ta matsa kusa dashi " yaya Khalid Kaine dama ka shigo dazu Wallahi ban taba tunanin zuwan ka ba yau,

Shiyasa ban kawo kai bane, "Hmm yace " kece bakisan da zuwa na ba Amma mutane da dama sun ga isowa ta har ma da wa inda akazo domin su, 

"Ko Hanan ? Ya fada tareda kallon ta cikin basar wa, murmushi tayi kawai " ya Khalid me kazoyi yau nasan kunada wasa gobe kuma inaso inga wasan ka,


"Wani murmushi yayi mai kyau " ina fatan bake kadai bace kikeson ganin wasan ba domin inason wasan ya burge mutane da dama,


"Ko juyowa Haneefa batayi ba tun Isowar shi wurin yi tayi kamar bata San da Isowar shi ba. nan  kuwa tana kallon Shigowar shi har ma da zuwan shi kusa dasu,



Hanan ce ta kalle shi cikin San dauke hankalin shi " ya Khalid bakace nayi kyau ba?
"Hmm Hanan idan nace zanyi bayanin kyan da Akayi a cikin wannan kwalliyar zan bata bakina ne sai dai inyi shiru kawai,



Isowar fahad ne ya katse musu maganar su domin Haneefa ya kalla' " Haneefa please minti biyu, 

"Wani irin hade rai Yayi ya kalli Samra, ke karku tashi a nan. Cikin bada Umarni,

Haneefa ta mike " muje ko' domin ita bataga dalilin shi na bata doka ba shiba ubanta ba'

Har ga Allah batason ganin fahad din Amma kodan tabar kusa da Khalid dole ta bishi,


"Wani irin tafasa zuciyar shi keyi kamar zata fito kirjin shi tuni idanun mazajen ya canja,


Hanan nata magana da Samra ina bai ko jinsu ya mike ya nufi su momy,


" momy kisa y'an matan ki su raka ni gida yunwa nakeji zuwa na kenan nayi wanka nazo nan,


"Saboda me zasu raka ka ai delu mai aiki na nan zata baka Abinci tunda baka sanarda mutane zakazo  


" Sory momy na" to kuje da Samra ko? "Aa bata iya girki ba kinsani kuma banason girkin mai aiki "
Haneefa kuwa don ita aka taru bazata bar nan gidan ba,



"Ummi ce tayi saurin magana " aa Hajiya sadiya shida kannen shi bari tazo ta mai girki a cikin gidan mu ai sun riga sun gama bidi'ar su ita da kawayen ta,


Mutum ya baro kasar shi yazo gida a barshi da yunwa,"Gaskiya kam yakama ta yasan yazo gida "ko zakaci girkin manya inyi maka?

Inna larai ke tanbayar shi cikin tsokana " murmushi yayi kawai ya waiga saidai zuciyar shi Saura kiris ta tsinke ta futo domin "ya gan ta ne a tsaye ga wasu samarin guda biyu bayan fahad  sunata magana a tsakanin su, 



" matsawa yayi cikin daure rai ya tsaya musu kamar Soja "hannu suka fara mika mai domin sun San shidin waye cike da girma,

Shikuwa ya had'e rai kamar had'ari " Kizo ana kiranki ko" 
"Wani irin kallo tayi mai domin ita dai batasan meye matsalar shi ba da ita' kuma wa ma zai wani aiko shi kiranta bayan ga Samra,


Amma dole ta wuce batareda ta ce mishi tak ba , Hanan na Ankare da shi tsab don haka kishi mai tsanani ya darsu a zuciyar ta ta kalli kanta da kyau " meye Haneefa ta fita dashi?

Gani Momy " kije kedai kiyi sha aninki Haneefa, "aa ya zakiyi haka " Haneefa je kiyiwa yayanki girki yanajin yinwa ,


"Abin wani iri ya mata wai yayan ta waye zstayiwa ma girki tukuna?
" ummi wane yayan?
"Khalid' 

Kallon shi tayi kawai tace toh domin bazata taba yin musu da,Ummi ba yanzu domin tasan ciwon kanta Ummi uwace ta nunawa sa'a dole ta yimata biyayya duk da ita ba Kwan ta bace,



" Ma'ma ce ta iso "Haneefa zo anaso a ganki' " toh kawai tace tabi bayan Ma'ma saida suka gaisa da bakuwar sannan ta wuce cikin gidan su,

"Cikin minti talatin ta hada mai meat source da kuma white macaroni domin ta taba jin momy ta fada cewar shine Abincin da yake so da kuma ketchup,


Don haka ta shirya cikin sauri ta fito kafin Ma'ma ta nemeta, Samra ta kira ta fada mata tayimai jagora zuwa cikin gidan yaci Abinci,

"

Ta sadak'ar  zata jita a kasa saidai akasin hakan ya fari domin ya marabceta a kirjin shi mai cikeda kamshin ni'ima da kuma kamshi mai tsayawa a zuciya,

Kuma ni duk tsiya mutum baiga komai ba ehem, ta Rabeshi ta fita da gudu "

"Haneefa ya tace kuma da tai makon ki na rayu da ita har zuwa yanzu don haka banida bakin gode miki sai dai inyi fatan Allah ya barmin ke ina ganin ki inajin dad'i,

****************
A kwance take kusan karfe goman dare Amma takasa runtsawa tarasa dalilin rashin baccin duk da gajiyar da tayi a yau,


Juyawa tayi ta zubawa Samra ido taga ita baccin ta takeyi hankali kwance,

Ta kalli bangon dakin idanun ta suka sauka a kan katon Hoton shi sanye da kayan kwallon shi  ya dora kafar shi daya akan ball,


Yayi matukar yin kyau, batasan lokacin da ta dora hannun ta akan sajen shi ba ta shafo,


Jin motsawar Samra yasa ta saurin komawa kan gadon ta rufe cike da tanbayoyi a cikin zuciyar ta,


" meke damu na ne haka? "Meyasa na kejin sabon yanayi a tattare dani?

Da kyar ta runtsa a daren har ta kosa gari bai waye ba,.

*************
Ma'ma kuwa tun Albishir din da  Dady ya fada mata ta kasa sukuni " ita za Ayiwa kishiya kuma ma da Asiya?


"Kuka takeyi sosai Hanan ta shigo itama a birki ce,
" Ma'ma kingani ko na janyo mana bala'i gashi yanzu Ummin Haneefa ta Auri Dady ga itama Haneefa ta tsare min duk wani farin Cikina,



"Tsawa ta buga mata" Hanan ki kula da zancen ki "Haneefa ya'ta ce jini na no matter what inason ta bakin Cikina daya shine Auren Aliyu da Asiya domin naasan  irin sonda yake mata, 



" kuma k'arki kuma sa min ya' a gaba daga yau tana cikin gidan ubanta,

"Saratu" Dady ya buga mata tsawa "ki rika sanin Abinda zaki rika fadawa yarinya itama y'ar ki ce banason ki raba min kan ya'ya'


" toh ai dole ka fadi haka "don wulakanci saida na tara jama'a ina cikin farin ciki zaka mun mugun Albishir,


" Wallahi Aliyu kaci Amana kuma Allah bazai barka ba, "kallon Hanan yayi " je dakin ki baby kinji,


Saida ta wuce sannan ya dubi Saratun "banda lokacin ki yanzu zanje wurin Amarya ta ya juya zai tafi sannan ya waigo " tanks for the special party dama shiyasa Banyi rejecting ba saboda daurin Aurena ,


Yau burina ya cika na samu Asiya don haka duk Abinda zakiyi kiyi yau kinci bonus..............✍

*YA'YAN GATA*



_written by Fenerh_

1⃣7⃣&1⃣8⃣

"Duk Abin duniya ya dami Hanan.kuka takeyi mai cin rai da tsantsar bakin ciki  gaba daya ta futa a hayya cin ta a daren ko bacci barawo ya kasa daukar ta,


" dole innemi iyaye na dole Ma'ma ta mayarda ni wurin iyaye na tunda har ta farayi min gori akan Haneefa,


Nima zan koma gaban iyaye na duk inda suke sai na nemosu koda kuwa basuda ko sisi,

**********.
Asiya " Inna larai ta kirata "na am yaya". Ina son wata Alfarma a wurin ki,
" wace irin Alfarma ce kike nema a wurina da bazan yi miki ba?


"Karkiyi wani tunani a cikin zuciyar ki " inaso ki janyo Hanan a jikin ki kibata kulawa irin ta uwa domin tana bukatar kulawar uwa Saratu ba uwa tagari bace na y'ar da dake da kuma halin ki zaki iya bata kulawar da ta dace,


"Shiru tayi kadan sannan ta ce" Yaya na dade da tausayawa Yarinyar saidai ganin Sam batason kusan toni yasa na ke kauda kai Amma zancen Gaskiya ina matukar jin ta a cikin jikina,


"Toh kiyi kokarin bata kulawa zakiji dad'in hakan kuma zaku hada hankalin iyalan ku idan kuka had'a kanku shima mai gidan zai samu kwanciyar hankali,


" nagode yaya larai Allah ya biyaki" sukaji muryar Alhaji Aliyu a bayan su,
Ummi ta ja mayafin ta cikin jin kunya domin tana cikin Adon ta mai daukar hankalin mai gida,



"Toh da Alkhairi ni zanje inkwanta " ta wuce tareda barin su a falon,


Tsaye yake yana kallon y'anda take rufe fuska "Uhm yayi murmushi irin na manya " Amarya ta ai sai Kizo muje daga ciki zaifi ko?


"Mikewa tayi ta yi gaba yana binta a baya saida ta shiga cikin dakin ta ta zauna a gefen gadon shima zama yayi gab da ita kafadar shi na gugar tata,


Hannu yasa ya janye mayafin " Alhmdulillah nagode wa Allah da ya kawoni wannan rana "yau gani ga Asiya a matsayin matata mai yafi wannan farin ciki a Rayuwa ta?

" burina ya gama cika "kin bani farin ciki biyu " na farko Haneefa na biyu y'arda da Aure na da kikayi na gode miki Asiya kuma nayi Alkawarin kare dukkan hakko kin ki da suka rataya a wuya na,


"Nagode Sosai ta fada a hankali domin ita kanta sabon shiga ce a wannan lamarin,

" muje Muyi Salla ko domin godiya ga Ubangiji, ta mike batareda musuba suka gabatar da Salla kafin sauran Abubuwa su biyo baya sai muce Asuba tagari Ummi🙈

**************
Asuba a idon ta  domin ta matsu gari ya waye don haka tana idar da Salla ta fito kitchen batareda sanin mai zata aika taba Amma kasan cewar tasan duk Abinda akeyi a cikin gidan yasa ta fara fereye d'ankali 


Ta dora ruwan zafin tea mai dauke da kayan kanshi kala kala masu karawa shayi Armashi,


"Koda gari yayi haske ta kammala shirya musu break fast tayi saurin gyara wurin domin ta koma daki tayi wanka,


Tana fitowa shi kuma ya shigo da d'an gudun shi na jogging suka ci karo tayi saurin yin baya tareda dafe goshin ta,


" Ashh ta fada ya zuba mata ido yana mai kare mata kallo "ita kuwa saida ta gama jinya ta kalle shi saidai yayi nisa wurin karewa halittar ta kallo,

Da sauri ta maida idon ta a jikin ta " tayi saurin dafe kirjin ta ta yi daki da gudu don kuwa daga ita sai y'ar rigar bacci mara hannu sai zanin da ta dora saman west din ta kasan cewar ta guntuwa,


Komai na surar jikinta a bayyane yake ya kuma gama kare mata kallo fes,


Saida ta fada bathroom ta datse kofar tana maida numfashi sannan ta fara murmushin jin dad'i "toh meye na gudu kuma sai kace naga  dodo,

" to ai shine ya sa min ido bayan ba kayan kwarai bane a jikina,


Shi kuwa Oga Khalid ai kusan suman tsaye yayi a wurin yana imagining irin kyau da kuma kirar da Allah ya bata she is gorgeous, 


"Gaskiya dole ma a daura mai Aure da wannan fulawar domin zatayi kyan ban ruwa,


***********
Ummi ce a tsaye ta gama shirya wa cikin wani less mai kyau  ta fito Amaryar ta,

Dady na tsaye ya nade hannu ya zuba mata ido saida ta gama fesa turare ta juyo ta ganshi tayi saurin dukar da kai 

Matsowa yayi gab da ita " ya rungumo kafadar ta ya zuba mata ido cikin wani irin yanayi,


Zaunar da ita yayi tareda kiran sunan ta cikin wani irin salo,
Asiya " me zan fadawa ubangiji idan har ba shaukin so bane ya d'ibe ni a daren jiya ba zan iya cewa ni Aliyu nine na fara karbar budurcin matata Asiya,


Kaina na cikin duhu "dama mace zata iya kaiwa irin tsawon shekarun ki batareda tayi disvirgin ba?


" ya Akayi kika haifi Haneefa idan har zaki kasan ce a haka ? Don Allah ki cireni a duhu,


"Murmushi tayi tareda daga mai rigar ta ta nuna mai kasan cikin ta " ka gani ba da Kaina na haifi Haneefa ba c.s aka yimin Sannan zancen budurci ni ai ban taba Aure ba,


Don haka na gama magana ta' wata wa wuyar runguma ya bata "nagode Asiya lallai ke ta daban ce Allah yasa Haneefa tayi koyi da halinki 


Kin biyani domin kin bani Abinda Saratu....saurin rufe mai baki tayi " Aa karkayi haka ka zamo mai rike sirrin mu mu duka kar ka zama mai fallasa sirrin matan ka ga junan su zaka haddasa fitina a tsaka nin mu,


"Nagode mata tagari da Tunatar wa Amma ni bazan fasa fada ba ni mai sa'a ne don haka ki rike a zuciyar ki kin gama mallakar Aliyu har ma da zuciyar shi,


" dariya tayi "toh na gode da wannan kyauta " bari in je in hada ma break fast, "saurin riketa yayi " ina ai ba inda zaki fita yau muna daki Amarci zamuyi bari ma insa a kawo mana break fast a nan,



"Abban yara Akwai yaya fa kuma Haneefa zata iya dawowa " ai Wallahi dole amun uzuri yau kika shirya zamuje umara da ga can dole mu shakata da kyau,


"Rufe ido tayi " Mun fa girma Abban yara, a ina yau kika girma duk rayuwar baya kina cikin kuruciya ne shiyasa bakya tsufa, ya fada cikin tsokana,


***********

Shirya wa sukayi kamar yadda suka saba yanzu wato shiga iri daya, Atanfa ce blue mai manyan fulawa anyi mata dinkin riga da sket na yayi ya Amshi jikin su musamman tauraru wa Haneefa ta futo kamar ka saceta,


Ta murza daurin d'an kwalin ta das ga kamshi na tashi ta kalli Samra "muje mu karya muje inga Ummi na,


" gulma zakije yi ko?
"Eh din zanje ganin daren farkon Ummi na ta fada tana kunshe dariya a nan take ta tuno da shekarar baya lokacin da sukayi Auren kawar su ,


Wata dariya tasa lokacin da ta tuna irin wautar da ta yi a lokacin nan ta bawa Samra labarin Abinda sukayi,


Itama tasa dariya " Shigowar shi cikin dakin yasa sukayi dif "ya zuba mata ido " precious ya fada a kasan makoshi,


Kafinya waske "ku fito ana jiran ku ko? Ya juya cikin basar wa " sum sum suka fito tareda Addu'ar Allah yasa bai jisu ba,



"Shikuwa yaji komai da ta fada wato shiyasa lokacin da ta ganshi yana fitsari ta sandare, shi a lokacin ya d'auka tsabar iskan ci ne Ashe  tsabar tsorata da tayine,


" yarinya kinada aiki kuwa Ashe ya fada yana wani irin murmushi ,
"

"Momy da Abba suna zaune har sun fara cin Abincin" ya'ta Ashe idan kina gidan nan na huta ba kamar wannan mai k'uyar ba saidai a girka taci gashi muna Kwasar gara,


Kinga baban ku ko magana bai iyawa,
"Murmushi tayi kawai ta janyo cup ta zuba ruwan tea kadan tareda janyo plate din d'an Kali ta zuba da source shima kadan,



" kallon ta kawai yakeyi "wannan d'an Abincin zataci? Hmmm " ni wa zai zuba min kun zuba naku kowa ya kyale ni,


"Jikin Haneefa na rawa ta mike tareda daukar plate ta zuba mai komai da ta shirya sannan ta zuba mai ruwan tea ta tura mai,


" ai da kinbar shi Haneefa idan bazai Ciba ya bari ai cikin shine,


"Momy na ni din? Eh kai tunda Abin naka har da iyayi, 
Baba mai gadi yayi sallama,


" ya gaida su kafin yace "Hajiya ana sallama da Hajiya Haneefa wai fahad ne,


" ido ta zaro tareda kallon fuskar Khalid da batasan dalilin yin hakan ba haka kawai taji wani iri kuma ma yanzu karfe tara ya biyota gidan mutane



Tuni ya hade fuska ya zuba mata ido tareda juya cokalin dake cikin cup din shayin ,



"Toh Haneefa tashi ki shiga dashi sitting room ko ? Samra shirya wa bako breakfast,



Mikewa yayi ya nufi waje batareda yace musu komai ba Abba dai yana jinsu saida Khalid ya fita yayi magana,


" me yasa yaron ki yakeson yawo yanzu kwanan shi nawa da tafiya yau ? Idan ina lissafi yau sunada wasa amma ya gudo ko meye dalili?


"OK koma dai meye dalilin zanyi maganin Abin dukan su dagashi har d'an uwan shi " yanzu ya dace a ce yaro karami kullum yana gantali akan hanya yau yana can gobe yana nan saboda rashin uwa,


"Toh ya zamuyi Alhaji d'an gin uwarshi suma sunada hakki a kanshi don haka dole Muyi biyayya mu kuma basu hakkin su shima laifin shine ya kamata yayi Aure Amma ya zauna kullum cikin neman kudi ko wa yake Tarawa?


Koma dai menene ni zan nemi mafita bari in tafi in shirya, bin bayan shi tayi itama Momyn ya rage Samra kawai tana Kwasar girki,


"Tsayawa yayi a barandar gidan har ta fito cikin tafiyar ta mai cike da natsuwa ta nufi wurin fahad,


Fahad na hango ta ya karaso jiki na rawa " haba gimbiya me yasa zaki mun haka ? "Jiya nayi ta jiran ki kunna waya Muyi magana Amma shiru na kasa bacci a daren jiya saboda tuna ninki,


Shiyasa Asubar farko naje gidan ku sai nayi rashin sa'a bakya nan shine Ma'ma ta mun kwatan cen nan nazo,


" Haneefa ki rufa min Asiri soyayyar ki tayi mun mugun kamu ki bani dama don Allah,



"In kika bashi hope zaiyi kuka dake don haka ki sallame shi kawai ya wuce domin Amince war ki shine mafi girman KUSKUREN da zaki tafka,


Muryar Khalid ce ta doki kunnen ta tayi saurin juyowa ta zuba mai ido cike da tuhuma,


" fahad ne ya mika mai hannu tareda cewa" me yasa zaka fadi haka? "Ni atuna nina Amince war ta shine mafi jin dad'in rayuwar ta da tawa ,


" gyara tsayuwar shi yayi "zata yaudare ka ne kawai malam don haka ka kama gaban ka kawai,


" Wani irin Abune ya caki zuciyar ta "me yake nufi da magan ganun shi,?
" Ranka ya dade toh kasa baki ta Amince min, domin na afka sosai soyayyar ta ta yimin mugun kamu,


Runtse idanun shi yayi yanajin wani Abu na mai yawo a cikin zuciya kamar zai fito tsabar kishi,

Kafin ya bude su har sun canja kala ya juyo cikin hanzari ya rungume ta tsam a cikin kirjin shi w'anda yayi dai dai da Fitowar MOMY da Abba,



"Sandarewa tayi kam ta kasa motsawa jiniyoyin jikin ta tuni suka tsaya cak daga Aikin watsa jinin da sukeyi,


Wani irin bakon Al amari ne ya ziyar ce ta w'anda bata taba experiencing irin shiba a rayuwar ta,


" Fahad kuwa tuni ranshi ya baci ga wani kishi shima da ya ca keshi, 


"Momy idanun ta a waje Abba kuwa zare gilashin idon shi yayi domin ya gasgata Abinda yake gani yau,


" saida ta samu d'an natsuwa ta fara kokarin karbar kanta saidai kash tana dago kanta idanun ta suka sauka a idon su momy,

."Wayyo kunya ina kasa ta tsage ta shiga shikuwa gogan ko a jikin shi 


"Innalillahi shine Kalmar da ta fito a bakin Haneefa yau ta shiga uku da Abin kunya me Khalid ya mata yau ? 

" meyasa zai aika ta hakan? Meye damuwar shi da ita ?

"Fahad ne ya kalle shi da idanun shi da sukayi jawur saboda kishi " Hmm kana nufin yin hakan da kayi shine zai sa na hakura da soyayyar Haneefa?

"Kayi kuskure ina sonta sosai kuma zan Auri Haneefa koda a kwance na ganku kai da ita,


Hannu ya daga cikin zafi ya kaimai duka " Abba ya buga mai tsawa "Khalid!"

"Tsayawa yayi tareda runtse hannun shi zuciyar shi na tafasa domin jin wani banza na hango Auren shi da Haneefa,



" Zo nan " Abba ya bashi umarni, ko d'ar baiji ba ya juya wurin iyayen shi ita kuwa ko motsi ta kasa yi a wurin tarasa ina ma zata sa kanta,



Momy ce ta matso wurin su "bawan Allah kayi hakuri kaji ka bari zamuyi hukunci akai don Allah,


" godiya fahad yayi ya wuce kawai cikin bacin rai " Momy ta rikota kanta a kasa cikin tsananin kunya " zo muje ya'ta kinji"!


"Cikin gida suka wuce suka bar shida Abba,

Wani irin kallo Abba ke aika mai mai cikeda tanbayoyi kafin ya bude baki " Khalid me nake gani yanzu?


"Cikin basar wa tareda nuna shifa namiji ne ba Abinda yayi da bai dace ba'


" Abba ba komai kawai na fada mai ya daina zuwa wurin ta ne yaki shiyasa nayi mai hakan,


Mamaki Al ajabi duk sunrufe Abba " Khalid tarbiyar da muka baku kenan ? Yanzu ka kama yarinya ka kwakume Amma kai nunawa kakeyi Bakayi komai ba ko?


"Yanzu meye na hana wani saurayi zuwa wurinta? " Abba banasone kawai "meye dalilin ka? " Abba basu isa tsayawa da samari ba, 

"Amma sun isa runguma? Sosa keya yakeyi sai yanzu ya fara jin kunya " Khalid shashancin da kakeyi kenan a kasar wajen?


"Aa Wallahi Abba ba na yin komai a can ita kadai ce na taba runguma, " Aure kakeso Khalid?


"Aure Abba? Ai ban isa Aure ba ni' ya fada cikin y'ar shagwaba " naji Amma Kasani saina hukunta ka akan Abinda ya faru yau ,



"Sorry Abba nasan hukun cin ka ba dad'i " kaje ka fara shiri ka koma kan sana'ar ka banason shirme da kuma shiririta .

"Toh Abba ya fada tareda shiga cikin gidan 


" Momy  batace komai ba suna shiga ta ce su fito suje gidan su kasan cewar weekend ne 
"Ku janyo y'ar uwarku a jikin ku ku daina wareta 


Shi zaisa ta ji itama ya' ce kamar ku " toh Momy ita ce take da matsa la munyi iya bakin kokarin mu akan hakan taki Sakin jikin ta itace take ware kanta a cikin  mu,


"Kuyi hakuri ko kune Zakuji ba dad'i idan irin haka ta faru don haka Kuyi mata uzuri kunji Kuyi hakuri da canjin da tayi watara na zata dawo hanya da izinin Allah,



Fitowa sukayi rike da jakun kunan su irin na yayi masu matukar kyau da ban sha awa suna hirar su " Addu'a takeyi a kasan zuciyar ta Allah kada ya kuma hada ta da wannan gayen har yabar Garin nan domin ya gama bada ita a gaban su MOMY,


Saidai suna fitowa shima zai shigo "ido ya zuba mata tareda aika mata wani irin kallo mai cike da ma anoni, 

" Soft body ya furta a hankali saidai tajishi sarai kuma tasan me Kalmar ke nufi,

"Ya Khalid yaushe zaka wuce? " kin matsu intafi ne "aa ya Khalid nasan kunada wasa yau " eh sun daga saboda bana nan sai gobe Amma yau zan wuce,


"Ina zakuje kuda bakwa gajiya da yawo? " gidan su Haneefa zamuje ai ba yawo ba' OK kije kiyima mudi magana ya kaiku ko,

"Oh mu da napep kawai zamu hau' " meye Amfanin motocin da ke gidan nan?
"Amma yaya ni Bangane ba Kaifa da ke sa ni shiga Amma yau kake hana ni?

" eh naji kije " tayi gaba tana dariya Haneefa ma ta rabe zata wuce saidai carab ya riko hannun ta mai laushi Kamar Auduga saida yaji wani shock a cikin jikin shi,.


"Ko dazu da ya rungume ta shikadai yasan me yaji wannan Yarinyar Akwai wani sirri atareda ita domin yasha rungume matan turawa Amma bai jin komai a tareda su to me yasa ita ko kallon ta yayi sai yaji sauyin yanayi?



" zaro ido tayi "Nashiga uku da wannan mutumin gashi Sam yanzu batason fitina kuma batasan dalilin da Sam bata iya yimai magana mai tsauri ba yanzu'


" matsowa yayi gab da ita ya zuba mata ido kafin yayi Ajiyar zuciya tareda fesa mata iskar bakinshi mai kamshi 


"Kalle ni" ya furta cikin dakewa "ta kalleshi sau daya ta kauda kai " me kika hango cikin idona "?

Ya kuma furtawa " shiru tayi "fitin tinu da kece sanadiyyar kunnuwar su " kuma ki sani duk Abinda kika gani a idona ba karya a ciki 


"Me nayi maka? Ta fada cikin shagwabar da ta kuma narkar mai da zuciya  " ke ko kikayi wani Abu,

Kuma kina nufin duk sakonni na a banza suke fita? "OK zan fada miki daga yau duk lokacin da nakuma ganin ki da wani namiji next time kissing dinki zanyi a gaban saurayin ba runguma ba,


Ya fada cikin wani irin salon rage murya " ido ta kara warowa cike da jin jinawa karfin halin shi da kuma rashin kunyar shi 


"Kara matsowa yayi gab da ita kamar zai shige jikinta " eh next time bakin ki zan tsotsa a gaban kowaye don haka ki kiyaye kula maza kinada miji"


Yana kai aya ya wuce batareda ya kuma cewa komai ba "bayan shi tabi da kallo kawai

Tsaye take akan Ma'ma dake zaune firgai firgai saboda rashin baccin da batayi ba jiya tsabar kishi,


" Ma'ma magana nakeso Muyi" "Hanan ki tashi mun akai kinji banason damuwa,
" Ma'ma ki fadamin inda kika dauko ni inaso innemi iyaye na,


Dago idon ta tayi cike da fitina "yazo gidan sauki kinji tunda kinzama butulu saboda mugun hali kin hana kanki sukuni to ki sani nima yanzu bana son damuwar ki na samu ya'ta jinina,


" wani hawaye ne mai d'umi ya biyo kuncin ta "
Mikewa tayi ta fisgo hannun ta ta nufi gidan Ummi ,

A harabar gidan taci karo da Inna larai ta hanka data zuwa gareta ,


"Toh gayyar tsiya na dade ina son in tanbaye ki ina kika samo ya' kika bani da sunan y'ar da Asiya ta haifa min?

" Na rasa gane gaskiyar zancen ki larai wacece ya'ta ta Asali Haneefa ko Hanan?............✍

1⃣9⃣&2⃣0⃣

" kuka mai cin zuciya Hanan tasa tareda matsowa jikin Ma'ma "don Allah karkiyi min haka Ma'ma,

" idan har saboda maganar da nayi ne kikeson jefa ni cikin RUD"ANI' don girman Allah ki yafemin bazan kuma ba,

"Kar ki jefa ni cikin mugun yanayi Ma'ma nasan ke uwace kina kauna ta kin bani gata kamar y'ar cikin ki a Rayuwa k'arki bata Aikin ki na Alkhairi,



" ture ta tayi ta tafi zata fadi Inna larai ta tare ta tareda zabga ma Saratu mari mai kyau a fuskar ta,

_Tsokaci A Rayuwa  shine so da dama mata mukan tafka kuskure mai girma ta hanyar nuna son kanmu, zaka ga mace na bakin cikin rike d'an da ba itace ta haifa ba, bama tunanin cewa a Rayuwa ba lallai sai d'an cikin ka zaka Amfana da shiba zaki iya haihuwar ya'ya goma da cikin ki  su girma batareda sun damu dake ba Amma sai wani d'an can da bake kika haifa ba ya rikeki ya kuma yi miki biyayya fiye da wa inda ke kika kawo su duniya don haka sai muce Allah yasa mudace"_


2⃣1⃣&2⃣2⃣

A cikin kwanakin farin cikine ke wanzuwa a cikin wannan gida, wato Dady Ummi da kuma yaran su biyu,

Gefen Inna larai kuwa Abubuwan sun yi mata cak domin gaba daya hankalin ta ya koma kan d'an ta tilo, ko wane irin hali yake ciki"?


"Haka ma gefen Ma'ma y'ar gayu gaba daya ta shiga tai tayin ta duniya ta tsaya mata cak ba miji ba ya'ya ga daular Amma batada Amfani " Gaskiya kwanciyar hankali yafi komai a Rayuwa,



Haneefa ce ke kwance a dakin ta "tunanin Ma'ma ya fado mata tayi zumbur ta mike ta nufi dakin Hanan da itama aka gyara mata kamar na y'ar uwarta,


" video call takeyi a kwance a kan gadon ta fara'a ce kwance a fuskar ta "Sallama Haneefa tayi,


" come in twins sister "ya Khalid ga sister nan ta fada tareda juya mata fuskar laptop din,

Dago kanta tayi kadan ta kalle shi, shima ido ya zuba mata batareda yace mata komai ba yana mata kallo irin na kurulla,


" Hanan tayi karfin halin cewa" sister ba gaisuwa? "Ta dan kakaro murmushi " ina wuni" kallon ta kawai yayi batareda ya Amsa ba,


"Hanan ta ja laptop din ta " ya Khalid yaushe zaka dawo na matsu bangan kaba" Hmm yace shima "Hanan nafiki damuwa domin inason indawo gida ayi wacca za ayi ina cikin damuwa,


" kallon shi tayi cike da tanbaya "? Yaya Bakada lafiya ne? " eh zuciya ta ke ciwo "


"Ciwon zuciya?" Ta fada da saurin ta, "eh Hanan inada ciwo a zuciya ta kuma maganin shi nake nema har yanzu ban samu ba,


" Amma ya Khalid ka fadawa su Momy mana' "zan fadawa momy na domin nasan ita din maganin kuka na ce uwace daya tamkar da dubu,


" Hhh na shaida hakan ita dan uwa ce ta kowa har ma dani da twins sister dina, "wai ya Akayi kuka zama twins?


" oh bamu fada maka ba ko ? "Anan ta war ware mai komai, girgiza kai yayi tareda murmushi " good, "Amma kunada ban banci'


" Sosai ma, Haneefa nada tsiwa' "I no that, ya fada a hankali batareda taji ba " ke kuma girman kai ko?
"Wai ni wane irin girman kai nake dashi?


" kinada taurin kai kuma while ita kuma takeda saurin fahimta, Bakida hakuri ' ita kuma duk tsiwar ta tanada hakuri,


"Kallo ta aika mai " yaushe ka gama karantar halayen mu haka?
"Inada saurin fahimtar halin mutum idan na ganshi koda sau daya ne,


" Hmm, toh bari inbar ka ka huta sister na jirana " shiru yayi kawai domin ji yake kamar yace ta juya mata ya kuma ganin fuskar ta  don saboda Shigowar ta yaja hirar ko muryar ta yaji zai rage damuwa,


Ta kashe laptop din bayan ta mai sallama ta juyo wurin Haneefa tareda riko hannun ta cikin jin dad'i,

"Am very happy sister, " yau ya Khalid ya yi hira dani sosai "kin San me sister?"" Aaa "ina matukar son Khalid,


" ina mai so mai zafi bansan lokacin da ya shiga cikin zuciya ta haka ba "nasan ba soyayya ta a zuciyar shi Amma inaso yasan cewa ina son shi,


" Am happy for you sister, Haneefa ta fada, "um nazo ne Muyi maganar Ma'ma'
" fuska Hanan ta turbune, "me yasa za ayi maganar ta?


" ya kamata Muyi Hanan "kina ganin ya kamata muma mu juya mata baya? " uwar muce ita fa"

"Ni banason ganin ta idan ke zakije ne sarkin iyayi sai kindawo nidai ina nan duk tsanar da ta nuna min sai yanzu da tasan ni y'ar ta ce zata zo tace tana son mu   ke ma din rashin zuciya ce tasa kike marmarin zuwa wurin ta,


" k'arki manta da irin wulakancin da tayi miki farkon zuwan ki,

"Hanan " karkiyi haka, "uwa ce idan muka kyaleta bamu kyauta ba musam man ke, ta nuna miki gata sosai ba irin gatan da baki samu ba saidai Kice tsanani wannan kuma ra ayine irin nata,



" toh ke kije nidai bazani ba, "Haneefa" Ummi ce ta shigo dakin,
Zama tayi a tsakiyar su tareda riko Hannayen su duka,


"Kinyi tunani mai kyau Haneefa nima inaso inyi muku magana akanta kuje ku kula da ita " Saratu na bukatar kulawar ku inaso ku nuna mata so zata dawo hanya mai kyau koba komai ai uwarku ce,


"Zakuso ace uwarku batada hali mai kyau?
" Aa Ummi Haneefa ta fada "OK toh kuje ku ganta"


"Nidai bazan jeba ita taje" "kallon ta Ummi tayi " sannan ta kalli Haneefa "kije Haneefa kinji,


Tafiyar ta tayi tabar Ummi da Hanan tana nasiha irin ta uwa mai zurfin tunani,


***************
Tunda ta katse kiran ya fada gadon ya rufe idon shi ganin Wankin hula na shirin kaishi dare yasa ya kira wayar MOMY,


" tana d'auka ya ce momy na Muyi video call,
"Dariya tasa za Ayiwa momy shagwabar kenan ?


" marai raice fuska yayi bayan ya kira ta "fuskar shi ta kalla' babana meke damun ka haka?


" naga ka rame min ne Bakada lafiya ne?" "Eh momy please kisa baki Abbana ya barni indawo Nigeria Wallahi bana gane komai a nan wasan ma banajin zan iya bugawa idan har ban dawo gida na ganku ba,


" kallon shi tayi cike da tuhuma "babana ka fada min gaskiyar lamari kaji"
"Momy ba komai" "wacece kakeso " Hanan ko Haneefa?


"Momy wa ya fada miki inason wata cikin su?
" nasanka  Babana  ka fada min Gaskiya karka cutar da kanka,


"Momy nidai ki roki Abbana indawo kawai,
" toh naji zamuyi maganar " "tank you my best momy,


" ya katse kiran tareda yin murmushi "I love you my momy, " itama murmushin tayi bayan ya katse kiran Jinshi takeyi har cikin jinin ta,

A zaune take duk ta fada cikin kwana biyu ta fita hayya cin ta saboda tsabar damuwa,


Shigowa Haneefa tayi cikin falon hade da sallama, "saurin dago kai tayi, kuka ta saki lokacin da tayi ido biyu da Haneefa,


" da saurin ta takara sa ta rungume ta "Ma'ma kidaina kuka haka gani nazo wurin ki'


" saida tayi mai isarta kafin ta yi shiru ta dago idon ta" Haneefa duk kun juya min baya kuda Dadyn ku "Aliyu yamun gargadin shiga gidan ku yace idan nayi zai sakeni,


" rungume ta ta karayi "kiyi hakuri Ma'ma bazaiyi ba yana cikin fushi ne kawai zai sakko,


" nafiki sanin babanki Haneefa baya fushi idan yayi baya sakkowa da wuri, 

"Ku ma kun guje ni Asiya nasan ban kyauta wa rayuwar ta ba na zalince ta, kidaina fadar haka, dama tun farko RUBUTACCE NE, 


Hakan zai faru da mu duka a Rayuwa kidaina dorawa kanki laifi " nagode Haneefa, 


Tashi tayi da kanta ta hado mata tea ta bata  ta kira masu yimata aiki tabasu umarnin gyaran gidan ita ta hada mata ruwan wanka ta ce taje tayi,


Kafin ta fito ta fitar mata da kaya masu kyau, wata Leda tagani ta janyo " Ma'ma meye a cikin nan?

"Kayanku ne keda Hanan kawata ta kawo muku nasan bazaku karba ba shiyasa na Ajiye kuma ga kayan da kika samu gifts can duka na Ajiye miki,

.ta fada cikin kalar tausayi, tuni jikin Haneefa taji tausayin ta murmushi tayi ta fada bathroom din Ma'ma da yaji kayan Alatu duk da baikai nasu ba tayi wanka ta fito,


Kayan ta bude ta zabi kala daya wani material less da akayiwa dinkin Senegal pink ta saka, domin kawai ta faran tawa Ma'ma rai,


" kuma taji dad'in Y'anda tayin ta kalleta "kiyi min kwalliya Ma'ma na' ta furta cikin shagwaba, 


" dad'i taji sosai ta janyo kit dinta na makeup ta fara tsara mata kwalliya ta fito fes ta ce to muje yawo Ma'ma ,


"Ina zamuje? " ki fara koya min mota inason hawa motar da kika siya min'
"Toh muje inkai ki wurin koyan motar ,

" amma da y'ar uwarki zataje da yafi, ta furta gwanin tausayi,
"Ai ita ta iya Ma'ma muje kawai,

Domin tasan Hanan baza taje ba, toh muje 
Makullin motar  ta dauka , suna fitowa Dady na fitowa shida Ummi ta rakoshi, 


" Haneefa ta matsa ta gaishe shi ya Amsa da fara a kafin ya daure fuska "ina zakuje? " zanje koyan mota ne nida Ma'ma,


"Kinason koyan mota shine baki fada min ba? " 

"Baban yara ka barsu suje itama fa tanada hakki akan ya'yan nan,

" wani kallo ya aika wa Ummi "toh nayi shiru saboda ke ya furta cikin murmushin masoya ,




" gaba Ma'ma tayi ta shiga motar ta tayar ranta a bace, Haneefa ta yi saurin binta ta shiga,


Suna fita Ummi ta juya itama cikin fushi batareda ta sallame shi ba, saurin shan gaban ta yayi " me nayi uwar y'an biyu zaki tafi ba ko sallama?


"Tsayawa tayi ta kalle shi cikin wata siga " Me yasa kakewa Saratu haka?
"Baka ganin nadama a fuskar ta, " don girman Allah ka sakko ka bata kulawa kar kasata cikin wani hali,


"Kasan ko Auren mu baka kyauta mata ba yakama ta ka sanar sa ita, " ni mace ce kuma ciwon ya' mace na ya' mace ne nasan da ciwo'


"Nade hannu yayi ya zuba mata ido" Asiya " kina sona kuwa?
"Ni kuwa ke sonka baban yara" "banga Alama ba, 

" mata na kishin mazan su Amma ke ko a jikin ki kina neman kai dani,  "aa Gaskiya nake fada maka kodan mu samu hanyar tsira gobe domin idan ka kauce hanya dukan mu zamu cutu,


" toh nidai a min Afuwa kar ayi fushi dani gimbiya ta, murmushi tayi  "bazanyi ba a dawo lafiya ta juya cikin gidan da sauri domin ta lura shi   yanzu yake kuruciyar shi,

Sunyi yawa sosai ita da Ma'ma n ta domin ta ji dad'i n fitar itama gaba daya bakin cikin ta ya yaye gidan su   Fahad suka nufa,

"Kayan kwalam kala kala masu aiki suka jera musu " bari inkira mutumin ki yana ciki,

Ta tafi da farin ciki, "Haneefa ta kalli Ma'ma" nifa bansan gidan su bane da bazan zo ba,


Ta fada tareda turo baki kadan, "ikon Allah toh saboda shi kikazo?
" ni nazo dake ne kiyi godiyar mota Hajiya luba na sonki sosai,


"Toh nidai mutafi kafin ta kira mayen d'an ta, " ido waje Ma'ma ta kalleta "Haneefa' kina hauka ne zskice mai maye? 

" ai ba aso dole duk nacin shi kinsan bazai Aureki dole ba,


"Fitowa yayi yana zubawa maman shi shagwaba" ai sai ka koma ka canja kaya surukar kace da kuma Amaryar ka suka zo,



"Turus yayi lokacin da yayi ido biyu da ita " tuni ya saki fuskar shi cikin fara'a us karaso natareda tunanin guntun wando dake jikin shi ba,


"Matsewa tayi kawai  don bata wani murnar ganin shi, "


Muje daga ciki Hajiya saratu " ta fads cikin rawar jiki,.


"Suna shiga ya zauna gab da kujerar da take kai,
" Hajiya Haneefa' ina fushi dake, 
Na baki waya har yau baki bude taba,


"Ta b'ace ne tun ranar da ka bani, ta furta cikin halin ko a jikin ta, 

" jiki na rawa yace "toh me yasa baki fada min ba? " ai da na kawo wata, "wallahi ina cikin damuwa sosai inason ganin ki Amma banason haduwa da wannan d'an renin sence din,


" Khalid sunan shi, ta fada a takaice cikin bata rai'

"OK what ever, yanzu meye maganar mu? " inaso inji ra ayinki a Kaina,

"Banida magana sai Abinda Dady na ya fada, " OK tanks zan fadawa momy na sai Aje ayi magana ko?

Sai ingan ubanda zai Aureka yaro da shegen shagwaba in Aure shi ince na Auri mace,
"A zuciyar ta taje Ayyana wa,


" Wani murmushi ne ya subuce mata lokacin da ta tuno da gwarzon namiji cikakke mai zati ya hada duk wani Abu da mace keson a wurin da' namiji,

"Kyau zati Aji da kuma nesa wato masu gida rana, 


Ma'ma na fitowa  ta mike domin jinta takeyi kamar akan kaya,

Saida ta biya  suka siya ice cream da yawa sannan sukayi  gida,



Ga ba daya ta mayar da rayuwar ta gidan Ma'ma domin debe mata kewa saidai ta shiga ta gaida Ummi da Dady ta fito,



" tanajin Dadyn zama da ita sosai domin komai yi mata takeyi saidai tayi bacci ta tashi kana ganin ta kasan tana cikin hutu sosai,

Hannun Haneefa ya kware sosai a tuki don haka yanzu ita takan tuka su zuwa school ita da Hanan idan sun taso ma basa jiran driver yazo daukar samra ita ke sauke ta sannan suyo gida,



"Sam Hanan taki kula Ma'ma Ummi tayi magana har ta gaji, 

Inna larai kuwa damuwa ce ta saka ta gaba mu samman a wannan kwanakin ko bacci bata iyawa tana mafarki da yaron ta sosai,


" a zaune suke ita da Ummi" har ma da Hanan a filin shan iskar dake harabar gidan "hirar tsakanin Ummi da Hanan ne Sam yanzu Inna larai batason yin magana,

" kafin ta bata Amsa ta zube kasa wan war "ihu ummi ta sanya suna rige rigen isa gareta,


" ta riga ta suma "Hanan ce tayi gudu domin kiran driver, 
Amma baya nan kawai dole ta fada gidan Ma'ma  



" Haneefa ta kwala wa kira, "suka fito kitchen tareda Ma'ma hannun su rike da spoon,


" Kizo Inna larai ta fadi' da gudu suka rufa mata har Ma'ma suka nufi wajen batareda sun ko dauki mayafi ba,



"Da taimakon Ma'ma suka nufi Asibiti da ita Saida suka karbeta sannan Haneefa ta nemi waya ta kira Dady,


"
Cikin minti kadan yazo shima domin ba nisa da wurin Aikin shi " likita ne ya kira Dady bayan ya  gama bata taimako,



Suka bishi office din shi ya cire glass ya kalle su,
"Kaine mijinta,?

" aa y'ar uwata ce "OK meye damuwar ta haka da har jinin ta yayi mugun hawa haka ? Domin jinin ta yakai matakin da zata iya kamuwa da ciwon zuciya,


" innalillahi wa inna ilaihirraji in, Ummi ta fada hawaye na zubowa,


"Dady ne yace likita wannan matsalar family ce, OK koma dai meye Kuyi kokarin dai daita komai idan har kunason lafiyar ta,


Fita sukayi Ummi tasa kuka, " lallashin ta Dady keyi "ya zaki rika mats kuka Asiya bayan ciwo ba mutuwa bane,


" Abban yara  ban yiwa yaya Adalci ba yakama ta inne ma mata farin ciki Amma Banyi ba na kyaleta cikin damuwa bayan batada buri sama da ganin nawa farin cikin,


"Ba laifin ki bane ya kamata ta fada mana ina mijin ta yake taki fada ,

Ni dai ka taimaka inne no mata su don Allah, " ki kwantar da hankalin ki zanyi iya bakin kokari na insha Allah,

Kwanan su biyu a Asibitin kusan kullum  sai Momy tazo itada Samra kai har ma Ibrahim yazo ya gaida ita,


Yau dai har da Abba momy ta kawo Asibitin domin ya duba ta,
Ba laifi jikin yayi kyau sosai ,


Dady  nan ya tarbesu cikin girma mawa tareda yi mai godiya, 


"Alhaji Aliyu kullum inaso mu hadu Allah baiyi ba Amma insha Allahu dole zanzo gidan Muyi magana mai muhimmanci ,

" ba damuwa  Allah ya kawoka, cikin dakin suka nufa domin duba Mara lafiyar,



Ummi da momy har sun fara koro hira a tsakanin su itama mai jinyar na zaune a kan gadon ta jingina bayan ta da filo tana cin aiba,


"Shigowar su yasa ta tsayawa cak da cin aibar domin yana nan y'anda yake bai canja ba sai dai shekaru da suka karu Amma tana ganin shi tasan hutu ya karu,



Saurin dagowa tayi da kyau bakin ta na rawa " Usman.... "Shima tsayawa yayi ya zuba mata ido cike da mamaki'


" Fatima" gaba daya hankalin su ya dawo kan su har ma dasu MOMY da Ummi, "kirjin Ummi ya buga " kardai a ce Alhaji Usman inyako ne mijin yaya larai?


"Fatima ina kika shiga tsawon shekarun nan? " Usman ina yarona?
"Ina yake? Don Allah ka kawomin shi inganshi,



" natsuwa yayi tareda daure fuska ya cire glass din idon shi ya zuba mata ido cike da tuhuma,


"Yanzu kikasan da kin haifi yaro? Bayan ya zama mutum " kin wofintar da kyautar da Allah yayi miki batareda kinyi tunanin yanada bukatar ki ba,


Fatima  wace irin uwa ce ke? "Kin zubar da kyautar da Allah yayi miki akan dalilin ki da ban San shiba,


" meye matsalar ki a Rayuwa da har zaki wulakanta   Aure na da kuma jini na da baisan komai ba,


"Alhaji" momy ta kira sunan shi ganin yana faman fada cikin fushi domin ta fahimci komai ta gano cewar larai itace matar mijin ta,



"Alhaji larai nada dalilin yin hakan don haka ka saurareta, " Hajiya Sadiya matsala ta dake kenan bakya gane laifin mutum Kinfi kowa sanin Abinda tayi min Badan Allah ya bani mace ta gari ba da bansan halin da yarona zai shiga ba,



"Dady ne ya dafa shi " Alhaji kayi hakuri yanzu muna Asibiti zuwa Anjima zasu sallame ta sai muje gida Muyi maganar wannan Abinda ya shafi family ne yakama ta  ta zamo mana sirri,


"Shiru kawai yayi tareda fita dakin MOMY ta bishi a baya da sauri domin tausar shi,

Kuka Inna larai tasa mai ciwo tasan bata kyauta wa d'an ta ba anya ma zai dubeta da idon uwa a gareshi,?



"Dafata Ummi tayi " Ya ya dama Alhaji Usman ne mijin ki? "Eh Asiya ikon Allah ne yasa baki taba sanin shi ba lokacin kina zuwa makaran ta kuma Inna ta rasu kika koma Rayuwa da Saratu bayan na fada miki megidana yaki Amin ce min in dakko ki,


" ban yi mai maganar ki ba saboda gaba daya banaso in dora mai nauyin ki saidai nayi KUSKUREN sakewa saratu ragamar rayuwar ki wadda itace Silar rasa dukan farin cikin mu nida ke,


Asiya d'ana  nakeson gani kamar inyi me a yanzu don Allah kusa Usman ya kawomin shi tun kafin in mutu inason ganin d'ana,


"Shigowa momy tayi tareda matsowa kusa da larai  " larai nasan kinyi komai saboda zumunci ne don haka na fahimce ki,..

"Zumunci da kika rike shine dalilin da Allah ya bani ikon rike miki danki da zuciya daya batareda wani banbanci ba ni nasan ikon Allah ne kawai ya cusa min son Khalid kamar d'an Cikina ko kuma ince fiye da ya'yan Cikina,


" Khalid na nan ya zama mutum sosai ya samu ci gaba yaro mai ladabi mai son y'an uwanshi zakiyi Alfahari da shi a yanzu don haka ki kwantar da hankalin ki zuwa gobe ma zai shigo Nigeria don yana Germany a can suka buga wasan kwallon su,



"Murmushi ne mai cike da farin ciki ya bayyana a fuskar larai, " ubangijin Allah ya baki ladan Abinda kika aika ta Hajiya sadiya Allah ya ci gaba da wanke miki kyakkyawar zuciyar ki,


Allah ya Albarka ci zuri'arki, ki fadamin yaranku nawa yanzu,?


"Yayan su ne dai sai Khalid sai yarku kinsanta da ma wato Auta Samra ta fada cikin fara'a,.

" Ummi ce ta kalle ta cikin farin ciki, "Hajiya Sadiya nagode wa Allah da ya hadani dake ke Alheri ce Allah yabar mana zumunci har Abada ina Alfahari dake,


" Ameen Asiya so gamon jini ne "Samun macs irinki Gaskiya a zamanin nan sai Antona, 



" Zama momy tayi sai hirar rayuwar Khalid taje fada ma larai ita kuwa tanajin dadi w'anda likita da kanshi ya bata sallama domin ganin har jinin ta ya sauka sosai da sauri,


******************
Alhaji kayi hakuri muje gidan idan Khalid yazo shim....."kimun shiru Hajiya sadiya yarona bazai je gidan ba kuma banason yasan cewa itace ta haifeshi  



"Karka raba da' da uwa " ita tafara raba Kansu bani ba yanzu bama bukatar ta a Rayuwa,

Sakin baki sukayi tareda mamakin shi  ya ci gaba da magana' "Dama na zone Muyi magana akan zancen Aure na, 


" na shirya Auren yanzu domin kakan nin Dady sunce bazai dawoba sai  na yi mata kuma inason yarona a kusa dani,


Momy tace,
"Kai madalla sun kyauta min da sukayi ma haka tunda munyi fama kaki yi tunfarko yanzu ai kayi Auren dole,


" Abba yace "ka samo mata ne? " aa Abba ai ka fadamin zaka nema mana Auren su Haneefa nida Khalid kwanan baya shiyasa na Amince,


"Toh na fasa yanzu kaje ka nemo Mata domin banason inkuma hada jini da Fatima yaran kanwarta ne suma zasu iya aika ta mugun halin ta don haka banason zumun cin ku da ita daga yau harka Hajiya sadiya,


" subuhanllahi Alhaji karkayi wannan danyen hukun cin zaka raba zumunci ne ?
Kuma karka manta har gobe Akwai igiyar Auren ka akan larai,


"Toh yau zan rubuta inbata domin  banason Khalid yasan itace uwar shi,


" Ibrahim ne yayi saurin kallon momy "nifa banfahimci me yake faruwa ba a nan gidan yau ku fadamin don Allah sannan Abba idan ka hana ni Auren Hanan Haneefa da Khalid fa,


" Khalid na matukar son Haneefa   yana sonta sosai Abba karka shiga tsakanin su koma meye  Kar yayi sanadiyyar wargajewar kyakkyawan Alakar dake tsakanin mu dasu,


"Ibrahim kamun shiru na fada banason Khalid yasan komai akai that's is final............✍

Gaba daya hankalin MOMY ya gama tashi domin megidan ta ya dauki  lamarin da mugun zafin da bata taba tunanin zai nuna ba,


"Gaba daya ya hana Samra fita ma saboda karta hadu da su Haneefa, 

Suma sunyi ta neman ta a waya shiru  bata daga ba gashi idan sun dawo school basa samun fita yanzu Akwai malamin da ke musu karatu a cikin gida,


Inna larai ta war ware tas yanzu sai dai damuwa kadan ta son ganin d'an ta, Ummi ce ta dauki wayar ta sabuwa sa Dady ya siya mata ta kira,MOMY,


" bayan ta Ajiye wayar ta share hawayen ta domin tayi mata bayanin duk Abinda ke damuwa tareda kwantar wa da Ummi hankali,


Da cewar kar ta damu insha Allahu komai zai zo da sauki zatayi iya bakin kokarin ta,

****************
Saukowar shi kenan malam mudi driver ya dauke shi zuwa gida yana hade cikin shiga mai burgewa irin ta ba haushe ga glass din idon shi ya karbi fuskar mai cike da kwantacciyar sumar da gayu ke yayi yanzu,



Gaba daya ya zama very silent duk wani kuzarin shi da fara ar shi sun ragu miskilan ci ne zalla ke bayyane a fuskar kyakkyawan matashin
Wato Khalid usman inyako ,



"Momy ta tarbeshi kamar koda yaushe Amma tun Shigowar shi gidan yasan Akwai matsala domin bai samu wal walar da ya saba tarar wa ba,



Samra ma tana gaishe shi ta koma dakin ta Abin ya bashi matukar mamaki, and yayi zaton ganin su a cikin gidan amma baigan su ba,



" bayan yayi wanka ya fito tareda nufar MOMY, "Mamana yau meke faruwa ne a gidan nan ? " me ya faru babana? Momy gidan ba wani farin ciki kamar yadda nasan shi,

Sannan na dawo yau ko idon Abba ban gani ba, bayan duk lokacin da zandawo idan har yasani yakan jira isowa ta, ina fatan ba laifi nayi ba,




"Bakayi laifi ba Amma yanzu ne idan bakaji magana ta ba zakayi laifi.
" Abba ya fada lokacin da yake shigowa gidan,,


"Saurin kallon shi momy tayi tareda mikewa ta tareshi cikin sauri, don Allah Alhaji ka sassauta mubi komai a hankali mu dauki komai cikin sauki sai kaga komai yazo cikin sauki,,.


" hannu ya daga mata tareda zama kan sofa, "Khalid ya gaishe shi tareda zamewa kasa ya zauna domin ya lura da yau Abba yana cikin fushi,,


" Khalid. "Na am Abba, " ni wanene a gareka?
"Kai babana ne '' good, " wannan fa? Ya nuna MOMY,

"Mama na ce' good, " toh inaso ka rike a ranka itace mahaifiyar ka har Abada domin itace ta Amsa suna uwa kuma ita tasan darajar ya'yan ta,


Banason kaje gidan Alhaji Aliyu badeggi koda da KUSKUREN hanya daga yau, "saurin dago kai yayi cikin kidima shida ke shirin zuwa a yau din nan ya sanar da Haneefa zuciyar shi shine Abba ke shirin samai ta kunkumi a tsakanin su,

Karfe Hudu na yammar  ya kirata a waya, "ina kofar gidan ku in shigo ne?

" Eh" ta fada tareda kallon  su Ummi har ma da Hanan dake Zaune a harabar lambun nasu,


Matsawa tayi kusa da Inna larai, "Inna ga Khalid yazo"
Jikinta na rawa ta mike "ina yake,? " ni yazo gani? "Ina ya tsaya?


Wani irin tausayi ta basu y'anda take rawar jiki, Haneefa ta zaunar da ita "ki zauna Yanzu zai shigo Inna"


Shigowa yayi cikin shigar manyan kaya yayi matukar haduwa cikin shigar yadi'n mazan  da aka yimai dinkin da ya matukar karbar jikin shi,


Idanu suka zuba mai su duka Ummi na wani murmushin jin dad'i haka ma Hanan, Inna larai kuwa kuka tasa tareda zuba mai ido tanajin wani irin farin ciki'


Khalid dinta ya zama mutum sosai, kasa daurewa tayi ta mike ta nufe shi tun bai karaso ba,



Haneefa dai Addu'a takeyi Allah yasa kar yayi wani Abun da zai batawa mahaifiyar shi rai,


Tsayawa yayi cak ya zubawa Inna ido wani irin yanayi yakeji a tareda shi , Abinka da jini yaji har cikin jikin shi cewa itace mahaifiyar shi,


Kuka takeyi yanzu mai kara bayan ta shafo kan sajen fuskar shi hawaye ne ke ta zuba kamar famfo,

"Kince inzo Amma kin kyale ni"

" na taron Ango ne our hero, fadawa yayi kawai ya sanya eirpice a kunne ya runtse ido,


Yanajin suna ta faman shaki yan cin su bai ce musu komai ba saida suka isa mota ta tsaya ya bude idanun shi ya fito,


Saurin kallon su yayi "ina kuka kawoni kuma nida nakeso in je gida banson iskan cin ku fa,


Nan ne gidan da iskan cin ku?
" eh muje ka gani,
Raba ido ya farayi batareda ya kuma cewa tak ba ya fara kallon estate din plat plat ne sun kai wajen guda shida uku a Jere uku a Jere gidane kamar wani d'an karamin gari,


Bayan katon gate din ga kuma fulawoyi da kayan marmari a ko ina su Apple mangoro su aiba lemu grapes gwanda a gefe ga kujeru da runfuna na shan iska kamar wani gini da ya taba gani A American da ya mugun burge shi har ya zana Hoton shi tareda nunawa Abba a lokacin,



"Welcome home my son" yaji yo muryar Momyn shi a bayan shi saurin juyawa yayi ya zuba Mata ido cike da mamaki ya nufe ta,


"Momy ina ne nan?
" rike shi tayi cike da jin dad'i "muje daga ciki ka huta kafin bayani ta jashi bayan ta yi musu godiya tareda basu umarnin ku shiga daga ciki An shirya muku lunch 


Daya daga cikin su yace godiya muke Momyn mu suka juya tareda nufar daya daga cikin plat din domin bin umarnin momy,



Suna shiga cikin Aljanar duniyar little Dady ya rugo da gudu " AUNCLE "
Tareda da'ne shi ya rungume shi cike da farin ciki " my Dady how are you?


Ya ce "fine  AUNCLE ka kawomin jirgin saman dakace zaka siya mun idan nakai wa Aunty Haneefa letter,?


Dafe goshin shi yayi ya  ma manta da wannan Alkawarin Amma yaro baya mantuwa,


Sakko sarkin magana, kabar AUNCLE ya huta, " no momy ki barmu na dade bangan shiba ya Akayi suka dawo dashi,


Is a long story ka yi wanka ka  ka ci Abinci,
Ya ce toh ni ban gane ba wai wannan gidan fa"? 


"Namu ne duka ba haka kake so ba tun farko?
Shiyasa Abban ku ya gina mana shi mu duka kowa da part din shi nakune kaida yayan ka karshe uku kuma nida Abban ku da kuma Abokiyar zama na,


" abokiyar zama kuma? "Eh Karka fada min bakasan zancen mu biyu Abban ka ke Aure ba,?


Kallon momy yakeyi cike  da mamakin halin ta wato tana nufin MAMAN shi ?

Shiru kawai yayi ya ce " zanyi wanka momy"
Ka shiga bathroom dina yanzu kafin ka koma gidan ka ko?


Ita ta nuna mai dakin dake dauke da bathroom a cikin part din ta yayi wanka ta shirya mai garan Abinci da yafi so 


Saida ya koshi ya fito cikin wani sabon farin yadi mai d'an banzan tsada ya na kyalli ya ce "bari inga wa innan gayun MOMY,


" hakan yayi domin sunyi kokari sosai,
Part din da suke ya nufa yana jiyo ihun su da kuma hirar su irin ta samari masu ji da nesa da kuma gayu,


Yana yin sallama suka buga mai ihu "wuuu ga Ango ya iso har ya fara shekin Amarci,

Toh  tunda kazo muje wurin Amaryar ko?
Wata uwar harara ya zabga wa mai maganar " ai banga y'ar da ta isa ba duk Nigeria  inje wurin ta ba da sunan zance,



Dariya suka sanya "harda Haneefa?
Mamaki ya keyi domin shi bai yi maganar ta da kowanne a cikin su ba,




" karku hada kowace mace da Haneefa, don fa ita ce ko sauraran ku bazan tsaya yi ba tuni zan wuce inje wurin ta ban ma bukatar ganin ku,



Sallamar Fahad yasa shi yin shiru wakili ya mike tareda sanya ihu irin na shaki yan ci,
"Ga Ango daya ya iso sai d'an gata kaga Angon Samra,



Saurin kallon su yayi kanshi a daure ya zuba ma fahad din  ido domin ganin da ya mai da farkon Shigowar shi ji yayi kamar ya tashi ya shake shi domin ya tsani namijin da duk zai rabi Haneefa da sunan so,


Saurin tashi yayi domin tunawa da sanadin zuwan shi Nigeria ya fisgi key a hannun daya daga cikin Abokan shi,


Batareda ya kalli gefen da fahad din yake ba duk da yaji cewar shine mijin kanwar shi wato Samra,


" yayan mu ba zamu ko gai saba ne? "Hannu ya daga mai kawai yayi waje 
Yana fitowa ya ci karo da Abba, tuni ya dukar da kai tareda bashi umarnin ya biyo shi ciki,



Saida suka zauna MOMY ta kawo ma Abba drinks ya gaida Abba kan shi a kasa, 



" Wato Khalid haka zamuyi dakai ko?
"OK ba damuwa kaje kayi duk y'anda kakeso bazan yi magana ba Amma Kasani idan har ka taka kafar ka kaje wani wuri  batareda izini na ba ban yafe maka ba",


Zabura yayi tareda mamakin furucin Abban tareda kallon idon Momy ko zatayi magana sai yaga Akasin hakan domin ita wayar ta ma takeyi hankali kwance,


Saida Abba ya shanye drink dinshi ya mike tareda shigewa ciki ya barsu,
Wani gumi ke wanke mai jiki gaba daya hankalin shi ya gama tashi,



Saida momy ta Ajiye wayar sannan ta kalle shi cikin murmushi " baba na ka kyale Abban ku tunda Bakason matar ba za a takura maka ba nasan me za ayi idan An kawo ta zata dawo gefe na da zama kaga ka huta da ganin Abinda baka so ko,


Tana kai wa nan ta mike ta shige tana Amsa wayar ta,
Ji yayi kamar ya kurma ihu ya momy zata mayar da shi jariri wai shi waye zai fahimce shi waye zai San cewa Haneefa rabin numfashin shi ce,




Yanzu Abba ya na nufin idan yaje wurin Haneefa bai yafe ba? Tashi kawai yayi jikin shi sharkab ya koma dakin da suke batareda ya kula su ba ya shige cikin dakin dake part din ya Afka gadon wasu hawaye na zubowa a idon shi masu matukar ciwo,


Shi kenan na rasata? Oh my Allah Help me, haka Yata juyi cikin tsana nin damuwa yana jiyo ihun mahaukatan Abokan shi da yakejin kamar ya shake su domin ya lura su Sam basu San halin da zuciyar shi ke ciki ba,


Ya gwada kiran ta har ya gaji Amma wayar taki zuwa , duk hankalin shi a tashe yake,

Masha Allah taurarin uku sunfito cikin Adon less purple iri daya dogayen riguna kowace da kalar gyalen da ya mata Haneefa fari Hanan pink sai Samra pitch color ,



Sin fito kana ganin su kasan "YA'YAN GATA ne fatar su na sheki saboda gyaran da suke Samu, ma'ma ta fito fuskar ta dauke da fara'a ta ce muje ku Sallami Asiya domin sai gobe zaku dawo nan,.



Ummi na zaune a falon ta suna hirar su ita da y'ar uwar ta suka shigo " toh Asiya zamu je inkai su Wurin Momyn su,


"Kaji ya saratu da neman dalili ni fa ku cireni a harkar ku keda Hajiya sadiya kuje can kuda ya'yan ku nima inada yarana,

" sudai  suna kallon dramar ne tareda tausayin Kansu ko me MOMY zata d'irka musu oho"?



Sunfito cikin tafkekiyar sabuwar highlander momy  haneefa da hanan  suna baya Samra ce a gaba kowanne yayi tsit  kamar ruwa ya cinye su ma'ma ma yau kyale su tayi domin ta lura salon iskanci ke damun su don haka ta manna musu saida suka ga sun isa hadadden estate din Samra tace,
.



"Nan ginin Abba ne ya gama ne? Idanun ta a waje Alamar mamaki domin ita tasan da labarin suma ido suka baza suna Kwasar kallo,



Saida tayi horn mai gadi ya wangale musu gate din ta shiga tareda isa gefen Ajiye motocin ta faka ta fito tareda daukar jakar ta,..

Sai ku fito shuwa gaban nin miskilaye ko wacce sai d'an banzan bakin rai takeyi ku gama nasan lokacin da nawa iya shegen zai tashi,


Tayi gaba bayan fadan suka biyo bayan ta kallon kowane part sukeyi domin ginin kamar w'anda aka dakko a turai
Aka kawo Nigeria,



Part din momy ta nufa cikin sallama " Hajiya sadiya ki fito ga miskilayen yaran ki kema ki taba,


Fitowa tayi da sauri fara'a fal fuskar ta "kai maraba da yarana kai cancad'i kin ga yarana sun haska sunfito Amaren su shar kal Saratu kinci kyauta a wannan gyaran toh aini ba Abinda zan musu kenan,



" aa ki kara ba ruwanki da nawa " toh shi kenan Abinda  wurina zasu dawo k'arki damu gyara sai sunce basa so,
