" dariya momy tasa "yanzu Hajiya Saratu ba kara duk Aikin da y'an uwanki sukayi sai kinje da kanki?


" eh ni uwar zamani ce ke ma ai ba karar kike ba don haka ba ruwanki, 
Saida suka yi dariya domin sai yanzu suke fahimtar halim ma'ma mace ce mai barkwanci sosai tana da zumunci ga shi Abin ta Sam bai rufe mata ido ba saidai Ajizanci na d'an Adam w'anda tayi nadamar shi sosai tayi matukar canjawa tazama mace tagari Abin Alfaharin ya'ya da kishiyar ta harma da mijin kanshi,




Momy ta nuna musu dakin da ta ware musu tace suje can kafin ta zo,
Suna shiga suka watsar da gyaluluwan Hanan ta mike ta ce ni bari in nemi kitchen ina son shan fresh milk,


"Ki dakko dani sister" Samra ta fada "bazan dakko ba karku fito ku d'auka wallahi Kaina na sani,
" jeki jarabatu idan Natashi zanje don inga ko ina na gidan ma,



Haneefa dai tsit kakeji saida Hanan ta mike zata fito tace" sister kayan ki sun kamaki da yawa ki mayar da gyalen kinsan zaki iya haduwa da idon kunya gashi kin ciko kema,



Harar ta tayi "nakaiki cikowa? Ke kinga jikin ki ne kalli kirjin ki kamar zai fasa riga kalli hips dinki ma " shekewa Samra tayi da dariya wallahi kowa ya canja duk da kuna mita,

Filo Haneefa ta jefa mata ta kauce"Hanan ta fita batareda ta dauki gyalen ba kanta ba d'an kwali sumar ta dake shan gyara tana kwance a bayan ta duk da batayi sumar Haneefa ba,



Shigowar shi kenan ya hangota tuni jinin shi ya tsunke ya kasa koda motsi har ta shige kitchen din da yana kallon saida ta kama kugu ta dade tana dube dube  kafin ta gano shi,



Jan kafar shi yayi ya nufi kitchen din shi ma tsayawa yayi ya zuba ma bayan ta ido domin tana fito da robar ta bude tareda kafa kai ta fara sha,


Saida ta kusa shanye wa ta Ciro tareda juyowa domin komawa dakin da y'an uwanta suke, 


Sakin gorar hannun ta tayi ta fara kame kame domin tayi matukar jin kunyar y'anda ya ganta 
Cikin in ina tace "ya, I bra him ina wuni?



kasa Amsawa yayi kawai ya kankance idanun shi yana kallon ta, saida ta kara gaishe shi sannan ya dawo natsuwar shi,


Ya kasa Amsa mata ta duka tareda daukar gorar milk din ta matso zata wuce ya kara tare hanyar tareda zabga mata na mujiya,.




" Amarya kin canja sosai meye sirrin? Tanbayar ta zo mata a wani iri don haka ta daga fararen idanun ta ta watsa mai su akan fuskar shi,



Domin maganar tashi ta matukar bata mamaki, ita a sanin ta wata magana mai karfi bata taba shiga tsakanin  su da shiba yafi shiri da Haneefa,

Saurin tsayawa tayi bayan har takai gab dashi tana shirin rabewa  ta wuce,
Ya kuma jeho wata tanbayar " Allah yasa Amaryar tawa ta karbeni da hannu biyu domin ni dai bazan taba kin taba 


Don tanada daraja da kika a idanu na tanada suffar da kowane namiji zaiyi Alfahari da Auren ta don haka ina farin ciki da matar da Abba ya bani,



Ina fatar ta zama uwa ta gari ga Little Dady da kuma yaran da zasu biyo bayan shi,


Ya juya kawai bayan ya d'asa  Aya batareda ya jira wani bayani daga gareta ba, idanu ta bishi da su tana matukar mamakin kalaman shi masu kama da gugar zana,



Haka ta ja kafar ta tanufi dakin da su Haneefa suke batareda kwakwal war ta ta gane komai ba,


Samra ta fara magana "kai sister yanzu haka kika dawo baki kawomin ba? shike nan bari ni in tashi inje. "eh kije ai kema kinada k'afa,



Haneefa ta diro daga kan gadon ta biyo Samra sister muje mu zagaya cikin gidan mugani,



Saida suka fara da kitchen suka dauki drinks Samra ta jata suka fita ta baya suna zagaya wa,




Yayi dai dai da Fitowar su fahad da sauran Abokan su idanun su ya sauka akan kya wawan y'an natan nan da nan fahad ya washe baki yana kallon su,



Cikin Abokan nasu yace " kai Abokina wa innan flawoyin fa? "A kunne ya rad'a mai suka kwashi shewa  irin tasu ta matasa yace " bari in isa wurin Amarya ta,



Su Sam basu lura da suba domin hankalin su ya dauku wurin kashe selfi a gefen garden din dake dauke da ita tuwa daban daban naci dana kwalliyar gida,



Saida suka matso Haneefa ta kalli fahad cike da mamaki me yazo yi nan kuma?




Saidai Abinda yafi Bata mamaki bai wuce ganin  ya gai sheta kai tsaye ba tareda washe baki kusa da Samra,



"Amarya irin wannan sheki ai sai ki hauka tani tun ba aje ko ina ba,

" Angaida Amaren mu bamu ga dayar ba ? "Fahad kayi introducing dinsu mana muna so musan waye Samra waye Haneefa  domin mungano a fuskar ka cewa wannan ce Samra,



Baki har kunne yace " sosai kuwa itace Amarya ta Samra wannan Haneefa,
Suka gabatar da Kansu daya bayan daya wakili da yafi kowa  magana yace "Sannu Amaren mu  Allah yakai mu ranar dama muna son ganin ku domin Muyi magana daku,



Ku hada mu da kawayen ku domin musan shirin ku,
Haneefa da Samra ga badaya  sun kasa cewa komai,


Ita Haneefa kare musu kallo takeyi ko itama Akwai Angon nata dana Hanan a cikin su?

Domin ta gano cewar Fahad Samra zata Aura lallai ta rabu da kaya domin daga ganin shi zaiyi jaraba,


D'if sukayi kamar ruwa ya cinye su Samra dai ta shiga tension yanzu fahad Abba zai hada ta dashi?
Bayan yanason Haneefa,


Bawai yanada makusa bane aa gayene d'an gata mai ji da kanshi har ma da Aljihu ita bata taba kawo shi ne mijin nata ba a Gaskiya,



Ganin bazasu samu ko da kalma daya ba wakili ya kuma cewa " kuje ciki idan kun shirya zaku hada mu da kawayen naku ko,

'Ina ba'ba  ai mu kwashe duka sai ka jira yaran da zamu haifa,


" Aniyar ka ta bika mugu nima zan samu matar inshiga daga ciki very soon, Allah ya nuna mana duka suka Amsa da Ameen,

Kallon fahad din yayi tareda mika mai hannu  " Am soy ina cikin damuwa ne brother,

*_Wannan shafin domin jin dad'in ku na mallaka shi gare ku kuci gaba da gashi_*

3⃣1⃣&3⃣2⃣

Suna shiga dakin suka samu hanan ta zabga tagumi, suka zauna suna maida numfashi kamar wa inda suka ci gudu,


Tunani takeyi me yaya Ibrahim yake nufi da magan ganun shi? Gaban tane ya buga dam  kar dai a ce shine mijin ta,?

"Kai ina tayi saurin  girgiza kanta tareda furtawa a bayyane, bazata taba yuwuwa ba Ai yasan tana son kanin shi ya za ayi ma suyi Aure da shi Sam bazai yuwu ba,


Dafata Haneefa tayi tareda tanbayar ta " Hanan lafiya kike mazurai?
Cewa kawai tayi ba komai batareda ta basu Amsa ba,



Anan suke fada mata haduwar su da su fahad da kuma zancen shine mijin da Samra zata Aura,


Tayi mamaki sosai itama, Samra dai ta shiga silent wannan wane irin had'i ne Abba yayi ? Toh ita taga nata mijin toh su Hanan fa ?



Shigowar momy yasa su natsuwa ta zauna tareda fara yi musu nasiha cikin hikima  irin ta mace mai zurfin tunani da kuma iya zama da miji,


"Hanan kece babba dake zanfara " ki kasan ce mai daukar kaddara ba komai da mukeso A Rayuwa yake zama Alkhairi a gare mu ba, zaki iya son Abu har cikin ranki Amma idan ba Alkhairi a tareda shi sai Azo ana da na sani,


Kuyi biyayya iyayen ku sunyi muku zabi da mazaje dai dai daku w'anda dukkan mu munyi farin ciki da hakan 
Har dai ni da zan kwashe yara na duka biyu,


Samra ta yi karab "MOMY waye zasu Aura? " yayun ki zasu Aura Hanan baban Dady Khalid kuma Haneefa, 

Wani irin faduwa gaban Hanan yayi ta yi saurin dago kanta da tuni sun ciko da hawaye Amma tayi namijin kokarin mayarda su gudun idanun momy,

Domin tasan da kunya tayi kukan kin d'an cikin wannan matar mai halacci da kuma kara,

Haneefa ma hakan ne ya faru tayi matukar razana da lamarin ta kalli y'ar uwar ta da Alamun tausayi hawaye suka zubo mata,


Nan take ta bude bakin ta "momy ni zan Auri yaya Ibrahim Hanan ta Auri ya Khalid don Allah,


Murmushi momy tayi tareda riko Haneefa " aa Haneefa wannan ba maganar mu bace zancen iyayen ku ne maza mu masu biyayya ne baza mu taba jayayya da su ba,


"Inaso Kuyi koyi da halin iyayen ku mata duka halin kara da Alkun ya babba baya taba ja yayya da maganar na gaba Kuyi biyayya zakuci riba zakuyi farin ciki kuma zakuyi Alfahari wataran,



Don haka banaso wata ta kuma cewa komai Allah ya muku Albarka 

Sannan ke Haneefa inaso ki sani Khalid bai San ke zai Aura ba kuma ina so dukan ku kubar shi nasan me zanyi Akai,



Ta mike tareda basu umarnin mikewa ta hada musu ruwan turare kowace taje tayi wanka,
Domin wani sirri ne na Amare dake huda jikin mace koda yaushe kamshin zai kasance a jikinta idan har tana  Amfani dashi time to time,



Gaba daya Hanan ta shiga damuwa yanzu mijin da take mutuwar so ya mata nisa tunda y'ar uwarta zata Aure shi,


*****************
Gidajen nasu kowace masha Allah ma'ma ta kashewa ya'yan nata kudi sosai  An cika musu gidan su taf da kayan zamani kala kala 


Dady  ya musu odar sabbin motoci kowace da choice dinta  ga filayen da ya mallaka musu har ma da company da plaza manya domin ma'ma tayi uwa tayi mak'ar b'iya,



Ummi nata ido kawai farin ciki fal zuciyar ta idan ta ga gatan da yaran nata ke samu A wurin ma'ma ta yi tsaye tayi Kane Kane a komai ta nuna ita fa uwace tsayayya da ya'yan ta zasuyi Alfahari da ita,



Sai Ana gobe Aure suka dawo gidan ma'ma lokacin y'an uwanta ne kab a cikin gidan da kuma kawayen ta  manyan mata yinin biki kawai Dady yace za ayi bayason kowace bidi'a,



Aranar ne kuma mama Fatima ta tare a gidan mijin ta uban  d'an ta tilo , farin ciki ne fal a zuciyar ta Dady ya shirya mata nata gidan shima kamar sabuwar Amarya,


Ummi tayi kewar tafiyar yayar tata sosai ga kuma ya'ya ma zasu bi bayan ta,
Duk wannan Abin Khalid kallo kawai yakeyi  ga Abokan su sun shirya walima ta maza kawai domin sun ga Alamar ba hadin kai daga gefen Amaren,


Fahad shikadai ke rawar kanshi  sun rakashe sun cashe  w'anda Khalid Sam yaki zuwa ko daya shi jira ma yake A gama Auren zuwa jibi ya bar kasar,

***************
An kawo saitin  Akwatuna kowacce saiti biyu da kuma makullin mota, Dady ma ya musu nashi akwatunan domin Al ada ce a Niger state duk wata y'ar gata iyayen ta na mata lefe fiye da gidan miji ma to suma Dady ya kashe musu kudi sosai,


Fadin irin kukan da kudi sukayi  zan bata lokaci sosai sai dai ku kwatan ta,


Aure ne Akayi shi cike da mutunci da kuma harkar girma Anyi shi cikin neman Albarka da kuma zaman lafiya ba wata bidi'a duk  jama'ar da suka taka naira dake Niger state  duk sun halarta  daurin Auren na IBRAHIM USMAN INYAKO da  HANAN ALIYU BADEGGI sai kuma  KHALID USMAN INYAKO da Amaryar shi HANEEFA ALIYU BADEGGI,
daga karshe aka daura na SAMRA da FAHAD,



W'anda Sam Khalid yaki ya halarta Abba kuwa yasa mai ido baice komai ba,

 Ana gama daurin Auren Dady ya shigo cikin gidan lokacin gidan ya cika d'an kam da mutanen ma'ma domin itace y'ar d'angi,



Amaren na can kuryar daki Haneefa da Hanan domin Samra na gidan su , sun makure kamar kajin da suka fada ruwa 

Cikin Ado suke kamar ko wacce Amarya y'ar gata cikin farin swiss less mai matukar tsada takalman  su iri daya,


Komai nasu ba banbanci sun fito a twins din su kamar ka sace su ka gudu saboda haduwar da sukayi,


Bazaka taba fahimtar wane hali suke ciki ba domin fuskar kowacce ba yabo ba fallasa   Hanan bakin ciki ne tare a cikin zuciyar ta,


Amma tayi kokarin boye damuwar ta saboda gudun bacin ran iyayen nasu tana kokarin dai daita natsuwar ta ne kamar yadda y'ar uwar ta tayi,


Haneefa kuwa gaba daya hankalin ta a tashe yake "yanzu Khalid ne mijin nata? Ita Gaskiya tasan ta na sonshi Amma bata taba kawo cewar zai Aure ta ba saboda soyayyar da y'ar uwar ta ke yimai,


Shiyasa gaba daya jikinta a sanyaye yake ba kuzari duk da momy ta fada mata cewar Khalid din bai sani ba don haka zata zauna tare da momy,


Hakan ya mata dad'i sosai kasan cewar tana matukar  tsoron haduwar ta da mayen mijin nata a yanzu domin tasan y'anda yake sonta ba raga mata zaiyi ba Sam,




Dady ya bada umarnin a fito dasu yanzu yana son ganin su,
Ma'ma ce ta raka su har falon Dady Ummi ma na zaune a gefen shi ita ma ta zauna a kusa da shi su kuwa Kansu a kasa,


" Toh Alhmdulillah  da wannan ranar  ta Auren ku ina matukar farin cikin ganin wannan ranar da zan sadaku da dakin mazajen ku 


Ina matukar sonku yarana Amma duk soyayyar iyaye akan ya'yan su bai wuce ka musu Aure ya'ya mata darajar su tana gidan mazajen su,



Ku kadai muka haifa naso Muyi Rayuwa daku har Abada Amma bazai taba yuwu wa ba,
Nasan kunada tanbaya akan Wannan Auren da na yi muku batareda na baku zabi ba,



Alhaji usman ya zo har gidan nan ya nemi komawar maman ku Fatima gidan shi domin darajar Khalid da kuma soyayyar ta dake cikin zuciyar shi duk da ya dauki lamarin da zafi daga farko,


Amma kasan cewar yana tareda mace ta gari tayi iya bakin kokarin ta ta nusar da shi har ya sakko,
"Bayan mun gama maganar Fatima muka yi maganar Auren Ku ya nemi Alfarma kuma nayi mai domin dama ni keda hakkin zaba muku mijin Aure,


Kuma ina fatan zaku yimin biyayya ina fatan bazaku taba bani kunya ba ina fatan ku zama matan da zamuyi Alfahari daku ina fatan ku rike mazajen ku da mutunci ba rashin kunya idan kuka min haka kun gama min komai A duniya,


Don haka Allah ya baku zaman lafiya, ya saka hannu cikin pocket din malin malin ya Ciro makullin motocin su ya d'an ka wa kowaccen su,




Suka fara godiya suna masu share hawayen dake bin kuma tunsu tun farkon fara maganar Dady, ya juya ya kalli Ma'ma,


Toh maman yara kinada Abin cewa? " aa Dadyn yara ai ka gama magana Allah ya saka da Alkhairi saidai ko Asiya,?


Ummi tace "wa ni aa Allah ya basu zaman lafiya kawai shine zan iya ce musu,
" toh ni fa yanzu zan wuce da su gidan mijin su,


Ma'ma ta zabura "haba Dadyn yara ana tsakiyar taro? " eh haka nakeson yi kuma shine dai dai kuci gaba da bidi'ar ku ni kadai zan wuce da yara na daga baya duk wadda ta ke so taje ta gansu a dakin su,



Kuka suka sa "yanzu saboda Allah tun yanzu Dady zai kai su sai kace marasa gata,


Sunaji su na gani aka tasa su gaba su Ummi ma kukan sukeyi har dai kar ma'ma taji labari 
Ya saka su cikin motar shi driver yaja mutane cike a gidan biki ya wuce da Amaren,



Gidan  MOMY ma cike yake ana ta shagali Samra da kawayen ta suna ta harkar su domin ita kam ta karbi fahad da hannu biyu saboda mutum ne me saurin shiga rai yayi nasarar  samun kulawar ta nan take basa minti biyu zai kira wayar ta,



A lokacin ne motar Dady ta kunno cikin estate din  saida suka faka ya nemi numbar Abba, yana d'auka yace "toh  Alhaji usman ga yaranka na kawo maka su,




Abba cikin matukar farin ciki ya baro Abokan shi da sukazo taya shi murna ya ratso cikin gidan tareda neman MOMY,




Saida Dady ya raka kowacce dakin ta kafin  ya kuma yi musu nasiha da sanya Albarka ya tafi,



Bayan tafiyar shi momy ta kuma dawo dasu gefen ta inda suka sami Samra da kawayen ta tana faman shagali,



Kallon ta Hanan tayi " Amma ke sister naga kamar murna kikeyi da wannan Auren,?

"Sosai kuwa domin my heart ya koya min sonshi cikin kwana biyu ji nake kamar mun dad'e muna  soyayya dashi,



Ta zauna tareda riko hannun Hanan " sister inaso ki cirewa zuciyar ki duk Abinda kika shirya mata ki rike ya Ibrahim wallahi zakiyi Alfahari da shi,


"Mutum ne mai hakuri baya son hayaniya zai bauta miki sosai zakiji dad'in zama dashi, kar kiyi tunanin don yana yayana ne , aa wallahi yafi ya Khalid saukin sha'ani ki duba fa shi ya karbeki da hannu bibbiyu Amma ya Khalid saboda tsabar taurin kai har yanzu yaki saukar da kanshi ya Masan cewa matar da yake so Abba ya bashi,




Ajiyar zuciya Hanan ta sauke bayan ta yi dogon nazari ta tuna ranar da suka hadu a kitchen da irin taushin magan ganun shi a gareta tuni taji rabin damuwar ta duk ta yaye,



Haneefa dai sai shiru domin tanajin bakin cikin mugun taurin kan na Khalid, 
Mama Fatima ta shigo cikin Adon Alfarma tana farin ciki ta zauna a tsakanin su,


" ya'ya na sun iso ina matukar farin ciki sosai, Samra tayi saurin matsawa kusa da ita "mama ni kuma da zantafi fa? Ta furta cikin shagwaba,


" ai tare zamuje ya'ta k'arki damu kafa ta kafar ki,
Momy da ta shigo ta ce lallai kuwa za a koroku ke da  y'ar taki ,


"Aiko da yayi Asara babba idan yayi gan gancin koromin Ya',

" toh ga yaran mu nan Fatima so nake ki rike min Haneefa nasan Khalid bazai taba zuwa wajen ki ba Amma idan gefe na ne zai dame ni so nake in koya mai hankali kafin inbashi matar shi,



"Toh ai kinyi dai dai maganin taurin kai yanzu ki duba fa ko Abincin ma yaji yayi mai saboda wahalar da kai,


Toh jiya saida yasha drip daurin Auren ma bai je ba yana can dakin shi a dunkule,



" Allah ya kyauta,
Tuni suka ware cikin y'an matan da yamma momy ta gabatar da walima inda aka gaiyato malamai mata fitattu dake Niger state,


Sunyi wa Amare  wa azi mai shiga jiki bayan An watse ne Aka fara shirin raka Samra gidan ta, to a lokacin ne idanun su sukayi clear ganin yai fa rayuwar su ta canja,



Suna kuka Aka danna kanta cikin mota sun so suyi mata rakiya Amma Abba ya hana, An rabu cikin kuka sosai,

*****"*************
A kwance yake a kan gadon wasu hawaye masu dumi suka zubo mai  shi kenan yau Haneefa ta zama mallakin wani "



Wayar shi tayi kara yayi saurin d'auka "kazo falo na yanzu, ya bashi umarni kai tsaye, bashi da zabi dole yayi saurin mikewa sanye cikin kanan kaya kamar ba Ango ba,



A zaune suke Momy mama Fatima da Abba sun sashi a tsakiya  Ibrahim na gefe daya kanshi a duke cikin girma mawa, 

Shima ya shigo tareda gai da su gaba daya batare da ya ko kalli gefen mahaifiyar shi ba domin har yanzu bai  kula ta,



Abba yayi gyaran murya tareda yi musu nasiha sannan ya sa ma Auren su Albarka " Khalid, ya kira sunan shi batareda damuwa ba,



"Nasan cewa gobe zaka tafi ko ? " tunda An gama daurin Auren ,so don  haka na nema maka visar   ka zaka iya tafiya goben,



Ya kalli momy "ki ramin ya'yan ki Sadiya,
Mikewa tayi cike da girma ma wa ta nufi dakin da suke sun kuma yin Ado cikin wani tsadadden yad'i na mata pink colour ga gyalen su iri daya komai dai tubar kalla masha Allah,


Ga kamshi na tashi momy ta riko hannun su zuwa falon ,
Tun Fitowar su Ibrahim ya zubawa Amaryar tashi da ta sauya mai tunani ido,


Wani farin ciki yakeji kamar yayi me ji yake kamar ya mike ya dagota cak kar tasha wahalar tafiya,


Shikuwa gogan ko dago kai baiyi ba saida suka zauna tareda bude baki suka gaida iyayen nasu kuma surukan su,



Kirjin shi ya mugun bugawa domin jin muryar wadda baiyi zaton jin muryar ta ba a yanzu,


Saurin dago kanshi yayi ya zuba su akan ta direct, tuni igiyoyin kuzarin shi suka tsinke gaba daya,


Abba ya kuma gyaran murya, " Ibrahim ga Hanan nan ka riketa da Amana nasan banida matsala dakai, sannan ke Hanan inaso ki rike Amanar takwara na usman kin sani cewar maraya ne yana bukatar uwa,


Uwa duk da yana samun kulawa sosai Amma kin sani yaro bai da burin da ya wuce ya ga iyayen shi a tare ba wato uwa da uba,



"Don haka  ka dauki matar ka ku wuce , ke kuma Haneefa kiyi hakuri mijin da muka yi miki zabi har yanzu bai karbi zabin mu ba don haka ki zauna tare da Momyn ku,



Tuni ya gano bakin zaren ya zabga mata idanu yana tsana nin farin ciki  dama ita ce aka bashi shiyasa suka kyale shi batare da sunyi mai bayani ba?


Lallai zaici uban su wakili da suka yita hidimar su batareda sun sanar da shi Gaskiya ba,


Abba yace "Haneefa kije ki huta nasan kin gaji sosai ko,
Mikewa tayi cikin takun natsuwar ta  har ta fita falon na Abba zuwa Side din mama Fatima,


Ido ya bita da shi ya ma kasa motsawa , Abba ya mike tareda kade rigar shi " kai wai yau a ina nake ne ?

Mama Fatima tayi dariya kawai ta mike ta fita domin tasan Alhaji da neman dalili tasan yasani tsokana ce kawai,


"Ko ma wacece ina bukatar hutawa tunda Allah ya bani mata na gari dole yau nima nagode wa Allah,


Momy ta mike tace toh Alhaji Asuba ta gari bari inje kasan Akwai sauran jama'a duk da suna gefen baki,


duk suka watse suka barshi a falon saida Abba ya kai kofar dakin ya waigo 
Ka  kulle min kofar ta waje idan ka fita, 



Ya shige yana kunshe dariya mama na taya shi,
Saida ya dade yana saka da war wara kafin ya mike yaja kafar shi da kyar,

Lallai ya taro match don ya hango ba karamin shirya mai iyayen sukayi ba,

Side din da aka nuna mai a matsayin na shi ya nufa kai tsaye w'anda sai yau da yasan ya mallaki Haneefa yayi marmarin zuwa gidan,



Tundaga falon farko yasan lallai ya Auro y'ar gata saboda komai nasu daga waje akayo  odar shi komai Anzuba musu Kamar a kasar turai ga kamshin turaruka na tashi,


Wani irin lumshe idon shi yayi ya nufi master bedroom Anan ma gadon ya burgeshi mai runfa irin na zamani yaji Cotton's masu matukar kyau  gadon yaji Ado gashi tafkeke Waldrop din ma Abin kallo ne wall to wall ga gefen mirror ma Abin kallo ne ka gefen Akwatunan su Anjera kamar wani kanti,



Haka ya gama leka ko wane dakin har zuwa kitchen yana mai farin ciki tareda tausaya wa kanshi,


.gefen momy ya koma ya same ta a zaune ita kadai tana shan coffee,
Ya zauna tareda yin shiru , "babana lafiya kake? 


Ya kasa cewa komai sai kallon kofar dakin ta yakeyi Amma yasan da kunya ya bude baki yace wani Abu,



Haka ya gama kwanar shi ya mike cike da sanyin jiki, " yanzu  ya nufi wurin baccin shi cike da na sani ,

Hanan suna  fita Ibrahim ya riko hannun ta tamau idanun ta a waje tuni jikin ta ya fara rawa har saida suka shiga part din su ya na kwakume da hannun ta,



Saida ya sada ta da dakin ta irin na Haneefa sak, ya zaunar da ita a gefen gadon ya tsuguna a gaban ta ,


Hannu yasa ya dago fuskar ta tareda Ajiyar zuciya,
"Alhmdulillah, " Hanan ina matukar farin ciki yau bayan shekara biyar da rasuwar maman dady,


"Ina bukatar kulawar matata da fatan zan samu kulawar da nake bukata daga gareki,?


Shiru kawai tayi domin ya mata mugun kwarjini da yasa duk wata tsohuwar rashin kunyar ta ta tsaya cak,
Yanada cika ido yanada sanyin hali,


" bari in kawo mana Abin motsa baki, da kanshi ya mike ya kawo musu naman kaza da fresh milk (ci muci kenan sunan wannan naman🥴duk wadda taci sai ta biya,)


Ya sata ta sakko kasa da kanshi yake bata kadan kadan, saida ta danci kadan tace ya isa,
Shima ya cire hannu ya tsiya ya mata Madara ya mika mata "ki sha ko in baki da Kaina ne?


Karba tayi saida ta shanye ta kuma mika mai cup ya ce " aa zata saki gaji idan kin koshi da yawa,


Mikewa tayi ta shiga bathroom din ta tayi brush  ta fesa mouthwash mai kamshi 
Ta fito,


Shima saida ya wanko bakin shi tareda dauro Al wala ya ce itama tayi,
Sun gabatar da Salla kamar yadda musulunci ya tanadar tareda neman Albarka dake gareta ya shafa,



Mikewa tayi ta koma kan doguwar kujerar  dake dakin ta kwanta  tareda dunkule wa wuri daya, ya zuba mata ido,
Kawai yayi dariya saida ya janyo laptop din shi ya zauna a gefen gadon ya gama tura wasu mihimman Abubuwa kafi n ya kashe  ya kalli wurin da take kwance,


Murmushi yayi ganin har tayi bacci ya mike ya rage kayan jikin shi ya sanya Short nicker sannan ya nufota ya cirata cak saida ya dorata a kan gadon ya zame hijabin tareda zubawa surar ta idanu yana mai hamdala,



Hannu yasa ya shafo fuskar ta kafin ya dawo kan shafaffiyar marar ta dake cikin riga ya zura hannun shi ya shafo ta ciki har zuwa cibiyar ta,


Taji touching din har cikin hanjin ta don haka saida ta motsa ya d'an tsaya kadan idanun shi har sun sauya,


Ya fara kwadai tuwa domin shekara biyar ba wasa ba ce yayi hakuri yau kuma ya jangwalo tsohon tsumi,



Wasan nin masu zafin gaske ne wa inda har yayi nasarar rabata da manyan kayan jikin ta ya rage daga ita sai bra da under wear,


Jin hannun shi akan kirjin ta yasa ta farka wa domin kuwa ba cafkar Arziki yayi ba saboda yayi matukar nisa,


Zabura ta yi tana shirin mikewa Amma ina ya riga ya gama rikicewa ya danne ta da karfin tuwon shi ya hade bakin shi da nata da take shirin   kurma ihu,


Tanaji tana gani ya ke juya ta y'anda yaga dama har zuwa lokacin da ya nufi babbar hanya saida ta gantsara mai cizo mai karfi saboda  Azabar da ta ziyar ci kasan ta.........✍

3⃣3⃣&3⃣4⃣

Zare bakin shi yayi cikin sauri duk da haka bai bar Abinda yayi niyya ba sai ma lallashi da roko da ya biyo baya,


"Please my Hanan ki yi hakuri mijin ki na bukace na yi hakuri ki tallafe ni ina bukatar kulawar ki please,"

Ya furta cikin wani irin salo mai cike da kasala da bukatuwa  Tanaji tana gani batareda tausayi ba ya murkushe ta mugu yayi y'anda yaga dama,


Tayi kuka har taba uku lada ya tarwattsa ta son ranshi ya  saida ta kasa motsi, to Abinku da yaushe gamo ya ma manta cewa ita fa sabon shiga ce saida ta kai  ko hannun ta  baya motsawa saboda  jigatuwa,.


Saida ya samu natsuwa kafin ya saurara mata lokacin tayi lakwas ranar duk wani bakin rashin kunyar ta ya mutu mutsu,


Koma wa gefe yayi yana maida numfashi tareda kallon ta cike da jin sabon  yanayi  wani irin sonta ne ke shiga cikin jikin shi,

Ya janyo ta zuwa jikin shi ya rungume ta tsam ji yake kamar ya mayar da ita ciki saboda so  da jin nishad'i a cikin jinin shi,



Wani bacci ne na wahala ya dauke ta shiyasa bai dame ta ba ya mike ya yi wanka  tareda Alwala ya fito ya sanya jalla biya ya tada salla ,

Kwana yayi yana jero Addu'o'i, saida  yayi sallar Asuba sannan ya mike tareda cire jalla biyar shi ya haye gadon tareda zuba mata ido,


Yana zuba murmushi mai tsada, a hankali ya dora hannun shi akan fuskar ta ya shafo,
Bude idanun tayi a hankali ta yi saurin rufewa ganin fuskar shi da tayi,


Wata y'ar karamar dariya ce ta kubce mai "tashi kiyi Sallah lokaci na tafiya' ya fada cikin kulawa,

 kasa motsi tayi ta kara dunkule wa cikin bargo, " oh sory nasan bazaki iya tashi ba bari in tai maka miki,


Ya nufi bayi ya had'a ruwa mai d'umi ya fito tana nan y'anda ya barta, blanket ya janye, tayi saurin dunkule wa wuri daya domin ba kaya a jikin ta, zubawa surar jikin ta ido yayi yana hamdala ya duk'a ya sunkuce ta cak zuwa bathroom din su,



Kuka ta fara yi tana rokon shi don Allah ya fita zatayi wanka duk ta nannad'e hannun ta a kirji tana boye mai......


Tsuguna wa yayi tareda ware hannun ta "me ye haka Hanan ya zaki boye min Abin da ya ke nawa kuma na mallake shi,


" bazan taba manta wa da wannan daren ba Hanan kin faran ta min sosai ina Alfahari dake nayi matukar sa ar mata ta gari Allah ya miki Albarka,


Duk da Azabar da ya gana mata Amma batajin wani haushin shi daga daren jiya zuwa yanzu wani girman shi take gani da kuma tsantsar kunyar shi,


Batasan lokacin da ta saki jiki da shi ba  saboda da da d'an kalaman shi, ya mata wanka  tas ya nad'o ta a towel har mai shi ya shafa mata,


 Ya Ciro mata kaya har da undis ta karba ganin yana shirin sa mata, ta ce "zan iya sakawa, matsowa yayi gab da ita " are you sure? Naga tafiyar ki har yanzu'..... Ko dai zaki shiga ruwan zafi ne ? Ko muje Asibiti?


Saurin girgiza kai tayi kawai ya na kallon ta wai tasa kayan, kasa shirya wa tayi domin ya kasa ya tsare, 


"Zan sa kaya' ta fada cikin yanayin shagwaba, Abin ya burge shi ya kar kata kai ya zuba mata ido cikin shaukin da ke shigar shi,



" ki saka mana ina so in kalli matana da kyau,
"Don Allah ka fita kaga Banyi Salla ba, " OK ya fada saida ya kwashe bedsheets din ya fita dashi sannan tayi saurin saka kaya,


Dakyar take daga kafar ta domin ba karamin zafi takeji ba shigowa yayi cikin shirin shi ya tsaya a bakin kofar ya zuba mata ido yana kara godiya a cikin zuciyar shi,


Saida ta matsa gaban mirow ta d'an shafa powder da turare ta shafa janbaki pink sannan ta juyo ta 


Sukayi ido biyu tayi saurin dukar da kanta, 
Matsowa yayi yana tashin kamshi ya rungume ta tareda zubawa fuskar ta ido,


Bakin ta yake kallo cike da shauki, har saida ya d'an gana da dira lips dinshi saman nata ya fara lasar janbakin domin Abin ya mugun burge shi,



Ganin hakan bazai fitar dashi ba ya kin kime ta sai kan gado  tana zille wa "please ya Ibrahim don Allah ka daina zanyi Salla ne fa,


Hawaye na zuba yayi saurin tsayawa tareda girgiza kai " no k'arki yi kuka jekiyi Salla Kizo Muyi bacci nasan kin gaji,


Sauka tayi da sauri ya tashi ya cire rigar shi ya Ajiye a gefen gadon yana kallon ta har ta idar  tayi Addu'a  Amma bata tashi ba,


"Inzo ne in dauke ki? Saurin mikewa tayi ta nufo gadon har tana hardewa ta laba ta kwanta batareda ta cire hijab din ba,



Murmushi yayi ya mirgina kusa da ita ya janyo ta jikin shi tareda cire hijabin ya dora fuskar shi a wuyan ta " sleep my sweet wife,



"Nasan kin gaji ne ko ? " ba Abinda zan yi miki bacci kawai zamuyi,

Da safe kam ita kasa tashi tayi  sai kuka takeyi duk ta rikita fahad har saida ya kira Hajiyar shi  tazo ta kula da ita cikin lallashi tareda yimai fada "fahad ka wahalar min da ya' me yasa komai sai ka nuna zalama ne,


Yana Sosa keya ya fita a dakin Momyn shi nata ban bami sai da ta kintsa ta tsab sannan tace " muje gida na sai kin warke tsab zaki dawo dakin ki,


Tayi matukar jin dad'i ta ko bita saida suka fito  ya gansu" momy ina zakuje?


"Zamuje  gidan ubanka ne sai ta warke zata dawo tunda Abin naka hauka ne, jikin shi narawa ya matsa kusa da Samra,


" please momy karkiyi min haka mana Wallahi zan kula miki da yarinya ki rufa min Asiri,


"Ai sai kayi Amma kafata kafar ta, ta tura Samra mota ya bata fuska tareda shiga gidan ya dakko key,


" Wallahi kafata kafar ta zamuje mu tare miki haka ya furta a fili bazan taba y'ar da ba ki dauke min mata ba duk da ya saba neman mata Amma daga daren jiya yayi Alkawarin ba zai kuma ba domin Allah ya bashi mace kamilalliya,

 Khalid kuwa ya yi kwanan tuna nin mafita gashi Abba ya mai booking plight a Gaskiya bazai iya tafiya ya bar Haneefa ba bayan yasan matar shi ce,


Wanka yasha kamar Angon ya fito har da hula yayi matukar yin kyau ya nufi   cikin gidan nasu,
A falon ya same su suna karya wa Amma banda Haneefa da kuma Mama,


Ya gaida su tare da baza ido ko zai hango ta Abba ne ya fara magana "Khalid banga ka shirya ba?


Shiru kawai yayi  kansa a kasa, " toh ka tashi kaje ka gaida maman ka ko kafin in kammala sai mu wuce,


Domin dai daga yau na riga na yi muku Aure ba w'anda zai kuma cimun Abincin gida kowa ya ciyar da gidan shi,



"Yaje wurin matar shi ta dafa ta bashi matana ni kadai zasu rika ba wa Abinci,


Kallon Abba yakeyi yasan da biyu yakeyin komai don haka yaja kafar shi ya nufi gefen Mama Fatima karon farko,



Tana tsaye a cikin falon rike da waya sai Kwasar dariya takeyi Ita da Samra ne wata irin riga ce mai hannun spaghetti ta matukar kama ta rigar ta sanya,



Kanta ba d'an kwali kawai tayi parking dinshi ne, tayi matukar yin kyau ga lallen da aka zana mata na Aure ya fita radau  jajur dashi Abinka da farar fata,



Tunda ya turo kofar falon yaci karo da ita don haka yaja ya tsaya yana mai bin matar tashi da ido,
Shi kuwa wace irin godiya zaiyi wa ubangiji da ya mallaka mishi matar da yake so A Rayuwa,



Maganar ta ce tasa shi y'ar dariya har ta jiyoshi ta yi saurin kashe wayar,
Tareda juyo wa,
Cikin natsuwa ya fara matsowa,


Ita kuwa tayi matukar tsorata da ganin shi bata taba tuna nin zai shigo nan ba, juya wa tayi zatayi dakin Mama yayi taku uku masu kyau ya rikota,



" haka ake tarbon miji? "Ina zaki tafi bayan kin ganni?
Dai dai ta tsayuwar ta tayi ta zuba mai idonta da sukaji mascara da eye liner, .


" ni banga mijina ba har yanzu bai son gani na don haka nima bana bukatar ganin shi,
Murmushi yayi kadan "ai shi mijin matar da Aka bashi yakeso shiyasa baki ganshi ba saboda basu fada mai sun bashi farin cikin shi ba,


Da sun fada min da ke ma yanzu bazakiyi wa su Samra dariya ba  kema da yanzu baki fito ba kin tsaya A nan,.



Wani irin saurin matsa wa tayi  lallai Khalid ai dama baida kunya, yanzu ita shi  aka had'a ta dashi ? Ta hadiye miyau,



 kara rungumo ta yayi, ya ru kun kume ta yana Ajiyar zuciya, idanun shi a akan fuskar ta ya furta" Alhmdulillah nagode wa Allah da ya mallaka min ke Haneefa,


"Kefa kina farin ciki?
Tsit tayi domin a tsorace take dashi, saida ya kuma magana" me kikeyi Anan bayan ga gidan mu can jiya ni kadai fa na kwana,


"Me zanyi a gidan da Sam ba a bukatar gani.... Ya rufe Mata baki' " aa ina bukatar ganin ki wata ce bana bukatar gani,


"Nidai muje yunwa nakeji kala kala, saurin kallon shi tayi " yunwar har kala gareta? "Yayi saurin Amsata " sosai ma, ko in fada miki ?


Da rashin wayau tace eh
"Kinga na farko Abba yace kowa ya ciyar da gidan shi toh inada nawa gidan kuma yunwa nakeji, wannan yunwar karama ce,


Sai kuma  babbar yunwa, da na dade ina cin Abinci ban koshi ba, kinsan ta?
Tayi saurin girgiza Kai, matsawa yayi da fuskar shi ya dora bakinshi akan kunnen ta,

.
" kwadayin matata nakeji tun tuni , yanzu dai muje side din mu,  kokarin kwace hannun ta takeyi tareda cewa,


"Mama na kitchen kaje ka gai sheta, " ke zaki fada min ? "Muje gidan ki shine magana ta ko in dauke ki ne?


Ta fara kokarin tafiya ya fisgota ta fada kirjinshi ta baya,
Fuskar shi ya dora akan wuyan ta tareda shako kamshin jikin ta,



Idanun ta akan wuyan kirjin ta dake cike da har kar Arziki ya farayin sama da hannun shi da ke kan west din ta,


Ganin Abinda yake da shirin yi yasa ta yin y'ar kara da kiran Mama"

Ai kuwa ta fito da sauri daga kitchen din rike da cooking spoon tareda tanbayar lafiya?


Ya yi saurin sakinta cike da kunya domin shifa kunyar ta yakeji sosai domin ganin ta yake kamar wata bakuwa ba Maman shi ba,


Saboda kunya yau har da gai sheta,  ta Amsa cike da mamaki da jin dad'i, tun zuwan ta bai y'ar da ba ma sun hadu sai jiya a falo Amma ji yau saboda rashin kunya har cikin falon ta,



Haneefa na ganin Mama ta matsa kusa da ita, ta kalle ta "ki wuce ki ci Abinci na kammala, haka tasa ta gaba  suka shige kitchen suka barshi a tsaye,



Kamar stature, wayar shi ta yi kara ya Ciro, sunan Abba yagani yayi saurin d'auka ya fita falon ,


" Bazaka tafi bane kabar ni ina jiranka,?
"Zan tafi Abba zuwa Anjima jirgin ba da wuri zai tashi ba,


" toh ni  na fita A sauka lafiya, ya katse kiran, 
Ai ba inda zanje wallahi sai sun bani matata haka kawai ingan samu inga rashi,



Daukar key yayi ya fita da shirin so ke plight din da yayi booking,
 Ba inda zanje,



Suna shiga kitchen Mama tayi dariya' "yayan ki baida kunya Haneefa kinga bai taba zuwa wuri na ba tunda na dawo gidan ubanshi ya dauke ni kamar wata kishiyar uwar shi,


Ta fada cike da damuwa, Haneefa ta dafa ta" kiyi hakuri Mama nayi miki Alkawari sai ya kula ki kamar uwa idan har muna numfashi,



"Allah yasa Haneefa da ko naji dad'i,
Suka shirya Abincin su sukayi kat  kafin suka gyara ko ina tareda taimakon mai Aikin Mama,




Saida suka kammala suka nufo gefen momy, 
Nan suka zauna hira cikin jin dad'i' Mama tace " Hanan kira,y'ar uwarki muji ko sun tashi nasa akai musu Abincin,



"Toh Fatima Abin har da daurewa karya? Ai da kin barsu can su  karata meye na kai musu Abinci?


" toh Hajiya ina ruwan ki da shiga sha anin  uwa da d'an ta? "Nayi shiru a sauka lafiya,


Wayar ta Haneefa ta kira taji muryar ya Ibrahim, 
" sister ya da damun mu da safe kar ki hana Amarya ta hutawa fa'


Saurin kallon su MOMY tayi taga Sam basa jinta ta mike tareda nufar hanyar waje,
"Wai baku tashi bane har yanzu?


" eh hutawa mukeyi Mungaji, "toh Abinci ne dama za akawo muku' " ai kuwa da kun kyauta saboda bazan bari tayi girki ba,



Dariya tayi domin jin y'anda yake ji da y'ar uwarta tasan baza a dauki lokaci ba zai yi nasarar cafke zuciyar ta


Tana ajiye wayar zata juya taji anyi sama da ita,
Kafin tayi wani yunkuri ya manne ta a jikinshi kamar wata baby,

Idanun ta suka fito saboda tsoro, saida taga zai nufi gidan shi da ita ta fara zillo, "ka sauke ni Mama ta aike ni'


" ba ita keda iko dake ba ni keda iko dake don haka muje gidan mu idan kuma kika ki Wallahi kasar zan bari tareda ke,


"Wayyo Allah ya Khalid wallahi ban shirya ba tsoro nakeji'
" Saida suka shiga gidan ya Ajiye ta' "tsoron me kike ji? " tsoron mijinki?



"Toh meye Abin tsoron?  inace ni ne dai kin riga kin gama gani na,

Kuka tasa sosai" ni banga komai ba kuma banason gani  don Allah kabarni intafi.............✍

Manna ta yayi jikin kujerar dake tsakiyar falon ya matsa jikin ta sosai kamar zai shige,
Cikin ta numfashin su na haduwa, jikin ta ya fara rawa tana in ina tace "Mama na nema na ya Khalid,


" oh har yanzu yayan ki kike kallo na? "Ni dai ka barni in tafi, ta fada kamar zata kurma ihu, 


Idanun shi har sun sauya launi ya cire rigar shaddar dake jikin shi ya sunkuce ta zuwa kan kujera three seater ya kwantar da ita tare da durkusawa a gaban ta kamar zai nemi gafara,.



" baby ya kira sunan ta dai dai kunnen ta" ina farin ciki har ban San inda zan fara ba, 
"Ina sonki sosai soyayyar da nake miki ta hanani yiwa Abba biyayya,


Kar kiga laifina bazan iya rayuwar Aure da ko wace mace ba, kece nasa wa zuciya ta zanyi rayuwar Aure dake, bazan taba kallon ko wate mace a suffar macen Aure na ba,


Kar ki hukunta ni a dalilin sonki ne, dawo da bakin shi yayi dai dai Lip's dinta dake sheki, ya sanya halshen shi akai ya d'an lasa kadan tare da dora yatsan shi yana shafo laushin su,



Wani irin tashi tsigar jikin ta ta farayi domin salon kashe mata jikin da yake yi ya  cire yatsan tare da dora bakin shi gaba daya yana tsotar Lip's din a hankali,


Har ya zura harshen shi cikin bakin ta, yanayin  irin salon da yake mata yasa ta gaba daya ta manta da wani tsoro taji Abin ya bala'in yi mata dad'i,



Ai wannan Abin dad'i gare shi shine mata kewa raki'?
Tuni ta cafko harshen shi ta fara tsotsa ita ma tana daga kwancen,

Wani irin zullo yaji wani Abu nayi mai har tsakiyar kai domin wani salo yaji na daban duk da ya saba kissing din ta Amma yau da yake ita ke yi mai Jin shi yake a gajimare,



Baisan lokacin da ya haye kan jikin ta ba ba tare da ya sake mata nauyi ba ya fara bata kiss mai wuyar mantawa  sun rike ce sosai,


Ganin kujera r Sam bata daukar su ya sunkuce ta sai bedroom,
Itama idanun ta sun rufe gamsuwa kawai take bukata, 

A hankali ya shinfide ta ya zame wandon tareda Jafar da shi ya fado gadon ya kara manne bakin shi tare da sanya hannu yana cire mata rigar dake hana shi ruwa gudu,



Saida ya zame rigar ya maida idanun shi kan kirjin ta dake tsaye kyam kamar na irin larabawan nan masu cikar kirji tana sanye da bra baka hade da wani short tight,
Saboda doguwar riga ce ta saka,



Kidime wa yayi tuni number six dinshi ta kwance ya dora Lip's din shi ta saman su ya fara lasa ,


Wani irin dad'i ke kara dibar ta tuni ta jike sharkab da ruwan ni ima, zame hannun bra din yayi suka bayyana gaba daya,


Ai karamar hauka ya fara yi mata   ya dora tafin hannun shi domin zame wandon da yayi saura a jikinta yaji shi sharkab da ruwan sama sai da yayi murmushi domin jin ni imar matar tashi,



Tayi nisa sosai cikin gajimare domin mugun ya iya haurawa da mace birnin maji dad'i ya iya kassara lafiyar ya' mace,

Don haka yasa ta manta wa da komai da kowa da kuma warning din su momy,
Amma lokacin da ya dora kirjinshi akan nata da nufin aika ta babban Aikin ai tuni kwakwal war ta ta dawo saiti,


Wani irin zillo tayi ta ture shi tare da dirowa kasa ta yi saurin wawurar zanin gado tana rufe jikin ta,
Daga kwancen ya dago ya kalle ta idon shi kamar w'anda ya kora corden,


"Haneefa meye ne"? Ya furta muryar shi na karkarwa,  ta kasa cewa komai sai dukar da kanta da tayi, tasowa yayi a hakan ba tare da jin kunya ba domin kuwa ya riga ya gama cire komai,



Ogan niya kuwa ai ta mike sanbal kamar titin Abuja haka ya nufo ta yana hada hanya,

Ta yi saurin kallon shi idanun ta a waje ta fara ja baya " na shiga uku na, ta furta a fili domin ganin irin tsayuwar da tayi kamar ice "don Allah ya Khalid Karka matso ni ka bari,


Jikin shi na rawa ya ce " me nayi miki yanzu? Nayi laifi ne? "Ni dai kasa kayan ka kawai don girman Allah bana son gani please,


Kallon jikin shi yayi ya danyi wani murmushin da dakyar ya bayyana saboda bukatuwa,


" kece ai sanadin tashin ta Haneefa kuma kece kawai zaki iya kwantar da ita,
"Ki matso gareni yanzu zata koma,


Kuma ai kin taba ganin ta tun ba yauba,  to meye Abin tsoro, jikin ta na rawa murya na cracking  " Wallahi ba haka na ganta ba nidai don Allah ka bari Wallahi Akwai wahala sosai akace, ta fada cikin rashin wayau,

" ni ka tafi Abin ka zan zauna tare da momy da Mama Kar ka damu,
"Bazan iya tafiya ba Haneefa ko nan da bakin kofa bazan iya zuwa ba idan babu ke,

Yana kife akan gadon ya dafe cikin shi yana murza kan shi a bedsheets din ta ta sulale ta fice da gudu sai part din Mama,



Ya dade a kwancen da kyar ya samu natsuwa ya mike ya fada bathroom ya sakarwa kan shi ruwa mai sanyi,


Akwai aiki tsakanin shi da Haneefa sosai da Alama zai ji jiki da ita domin ya hango tsoron shi tsantsa a idanun ta "me hakan ke nufi ?


Shi yau baijin zai iya bacci ba tare da ita ba a gadon shi ya tsokano wa kanshi fitina  muraran dinta,

Sai yanzu ma ta tuna da ta fita da waya kuma tana can part din su ba zan kuma koma wannan wurin ba,
Ta Ayyana a ranta,


"Mama na d'an zagaya garden ne' kallon ta tayi kawai amma bata y'ar da ba saidai kawai tace "toh kije kiga y'ar uwarki batajin dad'i,


" me ya same ta Mama? "Kije dai ki dubata kawai, saida ta shiga ta yi wanka ta canja kaya zuwa riga da zani  ta daura dankwali ta fito gwanin kyau,

Ashe Samra ta ja hakan tunda tayi a gadon uwar wani dole ayi a na uwar ta,



Suma gefen su hira suke cike da jin dad'i  Khalid yace "ya banga my boy ba?
" yaje school ko dazu ya damu maman shi zaije Aunty Haneefa nace ya bari har ya dawo school,


"OK nayi kewar shi sosai kwana biyu, 
Dama yanzu mun gama shiri zamuje can commissioner quarter's mu sallame su domin gobe zamu bar garin nan,


" kaida wa? "Nida matata zamu tafi, " Khalid Abba zai y'ar da? "Eh na fada mai tun jiya, " Alright Amma ya zancen y'an matan ka na can?


"Oh come on yaya kar ka fada taji mana ai na girma yanzu dole su kyale ni dama wannan y'ar nacin ce kawai ta rage,


" ka daiji tsoron Allah idan kaje musu da ya' "yaya Mubar maganar nan kar ta ji ka jamin matsala,


Dariya fahad yayi " lallai ana tsoron mutumi Yata, Sosa kai yayi tareda cewa "kasan munada fan's dole matan mu suyi hakuri,


" Gaskiya kam domin kuna da masu sha'awar kwallo sosai don haka dole matan ku su kawar da kai,

Gaba dayan su suka shirya domin zuwa gidan su Ma'ma  har ma dasu Samra, kowace a motar mijinta  ta shiga 


Haka suka jera gwanin burgewa kowacce ta kame a gaban mota,  
Haneefa da Khalid kuwa Abin ba sauki domin tukin ma da hannu daya yakeyi dayan hannun shi na kwakume da nata,


Tafiya suke a hankali kamar karsu kai  wani irin Annurin farin ciki ne ke bayyana a kan fuskokin wa innan Angwayen,

Har suka isa katafaren gidan na Dady ko wanne ya fito Ibrahim dai yana gaba Hanan na biye dashi Amma Khalid da fahad suna kwakume da matan su kamar ba gidan surukai suka zo ba



Gidan Ma'ma suka fara shiga tayi matukar farin ciki da ganin yaran nata da mazajen su ta sanya masu aiki suka taya ta baza musu kayan motsa baki,



Farin ciki fal zuciyar ta na ganin su cikin kwanciyar hankali,   dole tayi farin ciki,
Ta rasa ina ta saka su domin farin ciki,


Haneefa dai komawa tayi ta manne mata kamar ciki, wani irin kallo Khalid ke aika mata, nan ya tura mata text msg a take,


Tana budewa taga me ya rubuta, _"ni ya kamata a manne ma wa haka ba Ma'ma ba_ kallon shi ta yi ya daga mata gira daya,


Ma'ma ta mike "bari inbaku wuri Kuyi serving mazajen ku ko, ta wuce kawai ciki ta basu wuri,


Haneefa tayi wuf ta bita, suna shiga ta rungume ta tareda sa mata kukan shagwaba,  tayi saurin dago ta " meye haka Haneefa?



"Ma'ma wai fa Germany zamu tafi gobe shiyasa muka zo Muyi sallama, ta fada tana turo  baki,



" I kon Allah Haneefa, to dama ya za ayi ya tafi wata kasa ya barki a nan ke ba da' ba goyo, to Allah ya kai ku lafiya nayi farin ciki da hakan,


Dama Dadyn ku na shirya muku tafiya shaka tawa  daga can zakuje har umura to yanzu kinga ke kya je daga can,


"Ma'ma ni dai nafiso ya barni kusa daku, " eh lallai baki da wayo mijin naki zaki sallama aa turawa? "Toh a kul ki wuce ki bi mijin ki Allah ya baku hakuri,


" kiyi koyi da halin Asiya Haneefa kin San halin mahaifiyar ki kinsan bata dauki duniya a bakin komai ba shi yasa taci riba a Rayuwa,


Wani iri Haneefa taji da irin zancen Ma'ma a Gaskiya ta matukar canjawa sosai  Rayuwa kenan, ta rungume ta tareda cewa "ina sonki Ma'ma na ina Alfahari da kasan cewa ta daga jinin ki,


Hanan ce ta shigo tareda rungume ta itama " nima ina Alfahari da ke Ma'ma na ni kadai uwar mu maganin kukan mu,



Wani farin ciki ne ya ziyar ce ta har idon ta ya ciko da kwalla ta share tareda cewa "kai ku kyale ni haka yarona ko yarin Yata na nan zuwa ku na yaye ku ai, ta fada cike da nishad'i,


To me yafi wannan dad'i gata ga jinin ta A gaskiya sai godiya, " kuje wurin Dadyn ku kafin ya fita yana gidan Asiya,



Sakin ta sukayi suka nufi falon cikin jin dad'i mazajen nasu na zaune suna ta magana a tsakanin su Samra dai tuni tayi gidan Ummin,



Ma'ma ta fito itama "ku tashi kuje gidan Ummin ku " ina Samra? Fahad ne ya ce "Ai ita ta wuce can, 



Suma suka mike tareda nufar gidan na Ummi,

A zaune suke a harabar gidan na Ummi kamar koda yaushe idan taso shan iska kana ganin ta zakasan cewa tana cikin hutu da kwanciyar hankali,


Samra na gefen ta sai zuba take mata suma suka shigo da gudu tareda nufar ta, 

W'anda hakan ya matukar burge mazajen nasu musam man Khalid da ya tsaya tareda zabga ma matar tashi ido cikin tsananin burgewa,


Lallai matan nasu sune " YA'YAN GATA, uwa biyu ko wacce na ji dasu me yafi hakan dad'i a Rayuwa,


"Ku sake ni sai ku yada ni? Irin wannan runguma haka. ta fa tana dariya, Haneefa ta dora hannun ta a kan cikin Ummi " ya kake kani na, na matsu kazo duniya inganka,


Buge mata hannu tayi cike da jin kunya domin ganin surukan ta har ma da Fahad, Hanan  ma tace "nafiki matsuwa ingan mu da kani Wallahi ina sha awar yara,


" tab nafiki son yara ni jarirai na matukar burgeni, 

Wani irin murmushi Khalid yayi suka matsa tareda gaida Ummi cike da girma mawa,


Ta tashi tareda yi musu ja gora cikin katon falon gidan nata, itama haka tacika musu gaban su da kayan ciye ciye,



Kafin ta shiga ta sanar da Dady zuwan su,
Ta fito, Hanan da Haneefa suna manne da ita sun sata a tsakiya Samra na gefen su,


"Ummi kinga sun hana inzo kusa dake yanzu ko bayan sun kwace momy sun kwace mama yanzu kema sun kwace ki to ni bazan y'ar da ba,


" dariya sukayi Ibrahim yace "ke bakya girma ne ? Dady ya fito cikin murna " kai kai ku kyale mun mata kar ku dame  ta    bata da lafiya,


Da gudu suka tashi suka nufe shi suna rige rige,  Khalid mamakin su yakeyi gaba daya bai taba tunanin suna iya yin hakan da iyayen nasu ba,


Musam man Haneefa da ba wani sabo tayi da  iyayen nasu ba, 
Abin ya matukar burgeshi,



"Dady munyi kewar ka, " dafa Kansu yayi ya na murmushin jin dad'i, "nima ina kewar ku y'an biyu na, toh ku sakeni inje wurin ya'ya na,



" Dady mune fa yaran ka, "aa ba Gaskiya bane ku tunda kuka tafi ban ganku ba Amma su koda yaushe muna tare sai in basu fito ba,


Saurin kallon Khalid Haneefa tayi ya maka signal da ido daya ta koma ta zauna,


Saida ya zauna a kusa da su suka gaisa, sannan suma suka shige daga ciki tareda Ummi, itama ta fada mata dalilin zuwan su,



Tayi mata nasiha tareda su gaba daya, saida Dady ya leko yace su fito su tafi gidajen su yamma nayi,



Kamar karsu bar gidan sukeji, Ma'ma ta basu wasu hadin masu kyau, Samra harda rawar jin dad'i Haneefa kuwa Ayyana wa tayi a ranta,


" ai bazan sha ba haka kawai in sha ma Kaina damuwa Wallahi bola zaije, ta jefa nata cikin jaka
Suka nufi gida,



A gurguje 

Sun gama shirya kayan su tsab na tafiya sai waye war gari suke jira, wanda Khalid farin ciki fal zuciyar shi,


A daren kwana yayi yana faman danne danne a system tun tana tsoron kar yazo ya dame ta har bacci ya kwashe ta, 



Jin digar ruwa a kan fuskar ta yasata bude idon ta ta sauke su akan kyankyawar fuskar mijin nata,


Murmushin shi mai ban sha'awa ya aika mata tareda cewa "ki tashi ki shirya ai kin sha bacci sosai idan ma mun shiga jirgi ki kara yin wani baccin domin muna isa baki ba bacci,



Dole A biyani bashi, saurin Jan Abin rufa tayi ta rufe fuskar ta kawai, dariya yayi ya zauna tareda yaye a bin rufar ,


" ki tashi kafin  in sa jikin ki yayi tsami yanzu' kinsan kadan ba zai taba yimun ba shiyasa na ki kusan toki jiya na bar wa daren yau a Germany,



Saurin mikewa tayi ta gudu bathroom cike da kunya ya bita da kallo,

Sun bar kasar su ta gado  zuwa Germany, w'anda Haneefa tayi matukar jin kewar rabuwa da kasar ta, bayan nasihohi da  iyayen su sukayi mata,


Abba kuwa ya d'an kawa Khalid Amanar Haneefa tareda Jan kunne  sosai akan ya kula da ita, 

Tayi kuka sosai Alokacin da taga kawayen ta kuma y'an uwanta suna daga mata hannu zata shiga jirgi,


Hanan kanta taji ba dad'i ganin  y'ar uwar ta ta yi mata nisa, saida ta zauna a jirgi ya rungumo ta tareda lallashin ta sosai kafin tayi shiru,


Bacci ne sosai ya kwashe ta a lokacin da jirgin su ya fara shawagi a sararin samaniya ,


Hannun shi ya dora akan fuskar ta yana shafawa cike da farin ciki tare da jin dadi Mara misali wani irin Annuri ne ke bayyana akan fuskar shi,



Sun sauka lafiya a filin jirgin dake kasar  w'anda a nan suka samu wata d'an kare riyar motar da tazo taron su ,

Tayi matukar mamakin y'anda baturen yake ta bata girma kamar zai kwanta mata,



Yana rike da hannun ta gam suka shiga cikin motar " matsowa yayi dakyau jikinta,


"Welcome to Germany my wife, nasan kin gaji sosai ko,?


" eh ban taba shiga jirgi ba, "naga Alama ai lokacin da ya daga kika rirrike ni  a nan, ya nuna mata cinyar shi tareda nuna mata wandon shi da yayi squeeze saboda d'an ko shin da tayi,


Kawar da kanta tayi tareda murmushi, yayi saurin juyo da fuskar ta " oh baby ki rika yimun irin wannan murmushin I love it, .


"You are very beautiful Hanee, ya fada yana shafo fuskar ta da bayan hannun shi,

Am very lucky to have you as my wife, " yanzu ne zamu shinfida rayuwar mu Haneefa bazaki yi kuka da ni ba I promised,



Tsayuwar motar a bakin wani katafaren hotel ya sanya shi yin shiru tareda riko ta yana murmushi, "welcome  to my world madam,


Drivern da ya kawo su ya fara Kwasar kayan yana shiga dasu ciki,
Baza idanu takeyi domin karewa katon hotel din da kallo mai suna Vegas motel duk da yake dare ne Amma Garin kamar rana,


Yana rike da ita har zuwa part din da club dinshi ta kama mai w'anda yake first class,


Wurin yayi matukar burge ta kamar wani gida komai Akwai a ciki har kitchen idan kana ra ayin girki,



Kofar ya rufe bayan an gama shigo musu da kayan su ya matso kusa da ita,  rungume ta yayi tsam jikin shi, 


" Alhmdulillah. Ya furta a bayyane, bari Muyi wanka kafin akawo mana Abinci,

"Ban y'arda da wayon ba sai naga ni,  kamar zatayi kuka tace to ka tsaya zan nuna maka da Kaina basai ka wahala ba,


Ya ce " da gaske tareda Sakin ta Yanajin dad'i tayi baya tareda kokarin cire rigar ta saida ya sakan kance ta kwasa da gudu cikin bathroom din dake cikin dakin nashi,


Tana dariya, ya matse fuska kamar zaiyi kuka "haba baby ya zaki jamun rai please ki bude min kofar ingani,


Daga ciki ta ce " Bazaka ga komai ba, matsawa yayi bakin bathroom din yace "zaki fito ne ki sameni ai,


Gwara ki bude min kofar ma idan ba haka ba kika fito sai  nayi miki Abinda yafi haka,


bude kofar tayi tana cewa sorry na bude please karka yimin  komai,
Murmushi yayi  tareda cire rigar shi ya balle valet din shi tayi saurin ja baya ta rufe idon ta da tafin hannun ta,


Rufe kofar bathroom din yayi ya sunkuce ta sai cikin jacuzzi ya kunna musu shower,

 
Ta bude idon ta da sauri cikin tsoron jin ruwa a jikin ta, ta rirrike shi gam, ya samu damar zame rigar daga jikinta,


Ta kara k'ank'ame shi, a haka ya mayar da ita zir ba tareda ta iya yin komai ba, 


Jin ta a haka akan kirjin shi yasan ya gaba daya tsigar dake kwance a jikin shi ta mike,

 Kokarin dagota yakeyi Amma taki y'ar da domin haske ne dau a cikin bayin, 


Bakinshi ya dora a saman fatar kunnen ta ya fara lasa da wani irin salo tuni jikin ta ya sake,


Ya samu damar dago ta  ya zubawa kirjinta dake mike kyam ido  ga ruwan da ke sauka akan lallausar fatar jikin ta ya kara rura wutar sha'awar ta da ya takalo,



Tuni ya fara sarrafa ta cikin zafin nama gaba daya ya fita hayya cin shi bayaji baya gani,


Ji yake kamar zai yi hauka saboda bukatuwa,  jin yana kokarin yin aika aika a cikin bayi yasa tayi saurin kwatar kanta ta mike tareda janyo towel ta fito ta barshi war was a cikin bahon nasu ,


Yana nishi tareda mayar da numfashi, a tsorace ta dawo dakin jikin ta na rawa,

Ta nufi akwatin ta ta bude domin samun kayan da zata saka, saidai Abin mamaki,

Duk kana nan kaya ne a ciki irin na turawa, wato kayan da ya shirya mata kenan shiyasa ya hada musu kayan da kan shi,



Da kyar ta samu wata doguwar Riga mai kamar roba wadda da kyar ta  shiga jikin ta komai nata ya bayyana a fili kamar zata yage a jikinta gata dai da dogon hannu Amma mutum kamar Tsirara yake, 


Ta saman ta kuwa net ne ana ganin kusan rabin kirjin ta, 
Tayi matukar yin kyau kamar balarabiya ta fito saboda hasken ta kalar nasu ne,


Kallon jikin ta takeyi  ta gaban mirror ta kunna hand drayer ta busar da kanta, 


Ya dauki lokaci kafin ya iya fitowa daga cikin bayin daure da towel yana had'a hanya, 


Ya na kallon ta ya dauke kanshi,  "am going to die. Ya fada cikin zuciyar shi domin yau yakai karshen bukatuwa da ita Gaskiya,



" give me my phone ya furta  dakyar, ta Ajiye drayer ta nufi gefen da wayoyin su ke Ajiye ta miko mai,

Domin shi Sam baya cikin hankalin shi yanzu duk ya kidime jin ya fara nufar babban birnin maji dad'i,

Bayan wata biyu da zuwan su Germany ya nema musu gida har sun koma  idan kaga Haneefa kasan tana cikin jin dad'i da kuma daula domin tun zuwan ta baya bari tayi komai ko Abinci oda sukeyi,

W'anda suna haduwa suyita hirar rayuwar su suna bawa juna Shawara tareda labarin farin cikin da suke cike tareda da  mazajen su kowacce bata boye wa y'ar uwarta farin cikin ta,



Ummi cikin ta ya fara girma ya doshi kusan wata na biyar w'anda  Ma'ma ta gama shirin ta tsab har tsiya su momy ke mata,


"Saratu sauranki gadon karbar haihuwa don munga Alamar haihuwar ma ke zaki karba, 

Tayi dariya tace " ba ruwanku Ai bazamu haihu a nan ba bari cikin mu ya kara girma zaku nememu ku rasa,


Zaman Ummi da Ma'ma zama ne mai cike da Amana da kuma jin dad'i w'anda yayi sanadiyyar wanzuwar kwanciyar hankalin Angon nasu Alhaji Aliyu yana matukar Alfahari da matan nashi,


Gefe daya ga Amaryar shi ruhin zuciyar shi sai kuma uwar gida mai cike da tak'awa da kuma sanin ya kamata, 

"Meya fi wannan dad'i mata? Allah ya bamu ikon koyi da halaye nagari Allah ka cire mana son zuciya, kyashi mugunta ya Allah Ameen,


Haka take a gidan Alhaji usman inyako domin sa'ar uwar gida tagari mai kyan hali wato Hajiya sadiya uwa tagari mace mai son kowa hankaka maida d'an wani naki suruka ta gari, a gefe tareda Amaryar tata wato mama Fatima mace mai karfin zuciya da kuma zumunci,


" kai Allah kasa mu dace da halaye  nagari,

Abba ya shirya musu fita shakata wa a inda zasu samu su Haneefa a can daganan  su wuce saudiya su sauke farali,



Ibrahim da Hanan Abin ba a magana domin ta saki jikin ta dashi sosai ta manta da wani Khalid a zuciyar ta tace ai duniya mijinta yafi na kowa,



Haneefa na cikin farin cikin zuwan y'an uwanta  sun gama nasu shirin tsab suna jiran Isowar su,


Yau ya dauke ta domin fita shan iska suna manne da juna a cikin lambun shan iskar kasan cewar kasar ba ruwan wani da wani, 



Kamar daga sama  ta fado akan su ta tsaya tana huci kamar wata kumurcin maciji,


Haneefa ce ta fara ganin ta don haka ta yi saurin rungume shi tareda hade fuskar su ta hade bakin su wuri daya tana tsotsa,


Dama mai nema ai tuni ya bada kai ya fara mayar mata, tsawar da Vicky ta buga ne ya janyo hankalin shi har ma da wasu jama'ar,


A hankali ya kallo ta a wulakance  ya fara kokarin cigaba da Abinda yake Aikata wa,



A cikin harshen ta ta fara zuba mai masifa, tashi yayi tareda dago Haneefa yace "muje my baby nan hayaniya tayi yawa,



Riko shi tayi tamau tareda daga hannu tana shirin zabga mai mari  Haneefa tayi carab ta rike hannun ta,.

Maruka masu lafiya ta zabga mata tareda hankada ta baya ta nuna ta da yatsa " don't you dere touch my husband with your faulty hand he is mine.....


.....dafe kumatun ta tayi ta dago da niyyar ramawa yayi saurin matsar da Haneefa ya ce "k'arki bari hannun ki ya taba min jikin mata,


" mace mai tsarki ba irin ki karya ba da kowane jaki da doki yake bi ba wannan matar mutum daya ce ni kadai ke taba ta  banyar da ko wa ya tabamin itaba bare ke karuwa,



Wani irin kallo take mai "Inyako ni kake kira karuwa? Saboda nace ina sonka? toh ko Kaine Autan maza na hakura dakai har Abada Amma baza kaci bulus ba kaida matar taka,

Ta matsa kusa da Haneefa da zuciyar ta tayi matukar sosuwa  da lamarin  


" ke kuma da kike cewa mijinki ke kadai "are you sure? Kin samu saura na dai domin wannan kulawar da yake baki kadan ne a irin soyayyar da ya nuna min a soyayyar mu dashi,



Kishine mai zafi ya rufe zuciyar Haneefa Amma ta daure tareda matsawa gab da ita tareda murmushin karfin hali,

" eh ko ma a ce kun taba hadewa saboda tsabar kusan cin ki dashi ban damu ba saboda bana tareda shi bazan taba tuhumar shi akan rayuwar da bana cikin ta ba,


Amma ki sani ko yanzu Khalid ke bukatar wata macen zan bashi damar ya huta saboda tsananin soyayyar da nake mai Amma ba mace irin kiba  
Da namiji ya kirata da karya Amma kinyi asara,


Ta juyo wurin shi tareda sakar mai murmushi ta riko hannun shi "muje dear  in nema ma wata macen  ka huta,


Suka nufi wurin da ya faka motar shi suka shiga, yana barin wurin ta saki hannun shi ta juyar da kanta gefe  tareda hade fuska tamau wadda saida gaban shi ya fadi,



Ya kasa ce mata komai har suka isa gidan su,
A nan suka tarar da Abin farin ciki domin Isowar y'an uwan nasu,


Dama ya fada ma driver ya je ya jira su a airport,  ai da gudu ta nufe su suka rungume juna cike da farin ciki,


Samra tace " triple sister karkiyi wa Fahad Asara fa, tayi saurin Sakin ta ta dago tareda zuba mata ido Alamar tanbaya?


"Hanan tayi dariya " ke dai sister har ta kwashe ciki bakiga ta canja ba,
A nan ta sake ta ta nufi Khalid da gudu ta rungume shi "yaya nayi kewar ka gashi yanzu ka samu Haneefa ka manta dani,


Ta fada cikin shagwabar da ta saba yi musu, dariya yayi tareda kallon Fahad" brother in low me kake bawa kanwata ta zama haka?


Dariya sukayi yace "ai Abun sirri ne yayan mu sai mun zauna, Amma naga sister in low ma ta canja itama,


Hade fuska yayi tareda kai mai dukan wasa" wayace ka kallar min mata ? Ban fa manta da baya ba Fahad,



"To ba dadi ka rungume min mata A gabana, har ma da dungure mata kai, 
Ibrahim ne yace " idan kungama shirmen ku ku bamu Abinci munajin yunwa,



Khalid yace "baku koshi a jirgi bane? Ai ni matata bata girki saidai kuci na hotel, Samra tace " yanzu yaya kasan sarai muna zuwa  shine kuka fita yawon soyayya tareda manta wa da mu ko?


"Eh sarkin iyayi ko zaki hana ne? " ni na isa in hana yaya na hutawa, 



Abinci ya musu oda kala kala suka baje suna ci suna zuba hirar yaushe gamo  suma cikin dakin Haneefa suka koma suna ta zabga hira 



Anan suka baje mata sakon su momy harda kayan harka  daga Ma'ma masu kyau da inganci ta karba cike da farin ciki domin tanada bukatar su domin Sam Khalid baya bari ta huta,


Don ma ta kasance mai ni ima da  ta boni,  a nan take tana gama cin Abinci ta shanye wani tsumi mai kamar madara gefe da kuma dambun nama mai had'i duk ta cinye suka zuba mata  ido cike da mamakin ta,



Hanan tace "sister irin wannan d'uri zaki hadu da Khalid wannan tsumin kawai ya isheki Amma kina karawa da wani Abin 


Wallahi ranar da nasha shi saida na kwana da ciwon jiki my Khalil kamar zai ciremin gidan gwamnati,


Dariya suka sanya mata " Haneefa ta Ajiye sauran naman tareda cewa "na shige su Ashe da d'an naci na don ina ganin yafi kowa naci,


" naki kika sani bakisan na kowa ba, 
Sai da sukayi wanka suka canja dukan su sannan suka  fito,



Mazajen nasu na zaune a falon  Ibrahim na kan system yana faman aika sakonni Fahad da Khalid ne keta zabga muhawara akan wasan kwallon da  zasu buga wata mai zuwa 


Matan nasu suka shiga cikin burgewa kowace ta nufi gefen mijin ta Amma banda Haneefa dake shirin hora Khalid a ruwan sanyi, 


Shi kanshi yasan Akwai aiki a gaban shi yasan Haneefa nada kishi sosai duk Abinda tayi dazu yasan sai ya karbi query,



Gefe ta samu ta zauna tareda bude data ta fara duba sababbin shafukan sada zumunta,


Idanu ya zuba mata kamar zai hadiye ta ga shigar ta tayi matukar daukar hankalin shi dama ya bata hutun kwana biyu yaso yau ya huta gashi wannan shegiya ta Bata mai show 



Tashi yayi ya nufe ta zama yayi gab da ita yana shakar kamshin humrar da su Hanan suka zo mata da shi,


Ya lumshe idon shi tareda riko hannun ta ta fara kok'arin kwacewa,


"Zanyi kissing din ki a gaban su idan baki barni ba, ya fada a hankali, 
" muje ciki zamuyi magana,


Kin motsawa tayi tayi mai shiru 'kinajina ai ko? Ko in dauke ki ne ? Saurin tashi tayi bayan ta kalli Hanan datayi sanadiyyar dauke hankalin Ibrahim akan system din da yake aiki tuni ya maida hankalin shi akanta,



Taji dad'i har cikin zuciyar ta su Samra kuwa tuni sun nufi masaukin su  domin sauke gajiya ita da Fahad,


Wuce wa tayi cikin dakin domin gudun kar y'ar uwarta ta fahimci Akwai matsala atsaka nin su,



Bin bayan ta yayi bayan ya yi musu sallama shima "yaya ni na shiga daga ciki idan kun tashi  sai ku kashe kayan wutar,


" mune zaku bari masu gadin ku? Ai yanzu muma zamu shige ka rufe gidan ka da kan ka 
"Ai gidan mu a rufe yake ku dai kashe electronics din idan zaku shiga ciki,



Hanan tace " tafi Abinka brother ni zan kashe ka kyale my dear. "na  gode sister in low good night,



Samun ta yayi har ta fito daga wanka tana tsefe sumar ta da electric cumb saboda wanke shin da tayi 


Tana cikin wata Arniyar rigar bacci pink iyakar ta guiwa yana iya hango komai daga nesa  wasu irin mugayen miyau ya hadiye tareda nufar ta ya jona drayer ya zaunar da ita yafara kokarin busar da sumar,


" nagode ta fada tareda karbar drayer din ta farayi da kanta fuskar ta a hade ya zuba mata ido kamar zaiyi kuka,


Ya kasa ce mata komai haka ya wuce shima saida yayi wanka ya fito daga shi sai towel
Ya matso kusa da ita a lokacin ta gama kame gashin ta gefe daya ta fesa turaruka masu kamshin gaske,


Zame towel din yayi ya tsaya mata a haka, "tunda Anki yimin wanka yau a shafa min mai to,


Matsawa baya tayi tareda cewa " u can call your girlfriend for that,
Ta juya da nufin barin wurin, carab ya riko ta zuwa jikinshi ya manne ta kamar chewing gum,



Jikinshi yana gugar nata "Haneefa ni banida wata budurwar da ta wuce matata, " me yasa kikeso ki daga min hankali ne Haneefa?



"Please ki manta da komai muci gaba da rayuwar mu mai dad'i nayi miki bayanin komai tun farko ki manta da komai banida lokacin kowace mace da ta wuce  matata,


Zare jikin ta ta fara kokarin yi tana cewa " ai ta fadamin irin tarayyar da kukayi a baya  sai kuje kuci gaba ni na yafe kawai kuma zanbi y'an uwa na mu koma nija,


Maido da ita yayi jikin shi yana maida numfashi a hankali, "Haneefa yanzu akan magan ganun wata karuwa zaki nemi ki tada min hankali bayan kinyi min maganin ta,


" wallahi ko a baya mantaba aika ta zina da ita ba nasan munyi soyayya irin ta wayewa Amma ban taba yin sex da kowace mace ba sai ke Haneefa,


Ki y'arda dani, "ni bance sai ka rantse min ba Khalid Wallahi Haneefa tayi hankali na canja da Haneefar  baya ce sai nayi Mata rauni a yau  Amma a kiyaye gaba,


Ta juya ta nufi gado tareda rufa ta juya mai baya ta rufe har kanta,


Ido ya zuba mata yayi murmushi tareda nufar drowar kayan su ya nemi guntun wando ya saka tareda nufo gadon bayan ya kashe musu wuta ya kwanta,



Fir taki yardar mai a daren haka ya gaji fitinar shi ya kyale ta.



Sai bayan kwana biyu da zuwan su Hanan suka daga domin isa kasar Paris w'anda  har zuwa lokacin Haneefa da Khalid Abin ba sauki kuma sunbar Abin nasu a sirri ba wanda ya gane,



Saida suka yi kusan sati a kasar ne ganin Abin nata ba na kare bane ranar ya rutsata tareda bude mata wuta,


A daren bayan ta gama shirin ta kamar koda yaushe ta kwanta ya juyo da ita " me kike nufi da ni Haneefa,?


"So kike inje inbi  matan wajen da gaske ko? Idan da ban San dad'in Abin ba yanzu fa nasani don haka Wallahi yau ki bani hakki na ko ki kwana da tsinuwar mala'iku,



Dama itama ta gaji Jan Ajine kawai irin na mace don haka ba bata lokaci ta bada kai bori ya hau,


Aranar yayi kukan dad'i kamar zai suma domin irin tsumi da ta rika banka  da sauran su ta kara hadewa ciki da waje,


A ranar tasha Alkwaura ba iyaka   bayan ya natsu ya rika sanbada mata Addu'a da Albarka  ranar taga tarai raya,


" baby na daga yau nida mace sai dai su momy da Ma'ma da kuma su Ummi na,


Saboda kin hora ni da yawa don Allah k'arki kuma kaurace min zanyi hauka,



Dariya tayi tareda cewa "bazan kuma ba forgive me my husband, rungume ta yayi suka kwashi bacci mai dad'i,




Sun shirya isa kasa mai tsarki domin yin umara saidai kuma wani canji da takeji yanzu a jikinta......✍

A kwance ya same ta idon ta biyu ta na kallon saman  dakin ya shigo ya tsaya yana zuba murmushi daka ganshi kasan yana cikin farin ciki,


A hankali ya taka zuwa  kam gadon ya zauna tareda dora hannun shi akan shafaffen cikin ta "he can't wait to see  his child,


Kallon shi tayi idanun ta ya ciko da hawaye ta tashi zaune tareda  goge kwallar kar ta fito,



" bakiyi bacci ba maman baby na ? Ya fada cikin jin dad'i  ya janyo ta zuwa jikin shi ya rungume ta "baby na kin gama biya na, you have no I dear how happy I am today,


" kin faranta min zuciya saboda ina matukar burin inganni da d'an Cikina inason ganin  kwai na a duniya  muma so mu cika masu Abba gida da jikoki y'anda  basu haifa da yawa ba mu mu haifa musu da yawa,


Dagowa  tayi " hubby yanzu ya maganar karatu na? Kallon ta yayi cike da Alamar tanbaya, ta ci gaba,

"Yanzu zan fara haihuwa ta yaya zanyi karatu ? Fuskar shi ta canja gaba daya ya d'an ture ta daga jikin shi " yanzu Haneefa saboda karatun kine dama yasa gaba daya kika kasa farin ciki da karuwar da zamu samu?


Fuskar shi ta kara canjawa "saboda karatu kike nuna bakin ciki akan kwai na dake marar ki  shiyasa kika kasa farin ciki ? I tought  you will be happy for me ?


Wani irin bacin rai ne ya ziyar ce shi " Haneefa dama bakya sona har haka ? Saboda da kina sona zakiyi farin ciki da cikin jikin ki bakisan irin farin cikin da nakeji ba Haneefa a yau 


Farin cikin da na tsinci Kaina a yau zan iya kyautar da duk dukiyar da na mallaka saboda farin cikin ki Haneefa "me zanyi da dukiyar da na tara idan har ban haifi  yara na ba,


Ki fada min Haneefa meye laifi na da bakya sona haka? Tasowa tayi cikin rashin jin dadi ta nufe shi yayi saurin daga mata hannu Alamar ta tsaya a nan,


Jikin ta na rawa ta fara cewa " karkayi wani tunani hubby Wallahi ba wai banason haihuwar bane naga da na fara Ajiye yara karatun ba zai yuwu ba,


Wani irin kallo yayi mata "su Hanan da suke da cikin haka kika ga sunyi? Kodan ni bakya kauna ta shiyasa bakyason hada zuri'a dani?


" Haneefa wane irin ilimi ne zaki nema a duniya da  yafi yaran da zaki haifa mina ? Ya'ya sune cikon jin dad'in Rayuwa  idan ka same su burinka ya gama cika a duniya,



"Amma Haneefa kina,nuna min cewar ke bakya farin ciki da kyautar da Allah ya bani,


Wani irin bakin ciki ne ya kara turnike shi tuni ya juya ya bar dakin zuwa waje duk wani farin cikin shi ya rushe cikin lokaci kadan Haneefa ta bata mai rai ta hanyar nuna mai ita Sam bata shirya haihuwa da shi ba,



Fita hanyar ta yayi gaba daya ya ci gaba da bautar Allah tareda Addu'a akan Allah ya kawo min d'an shi ko y'ar shi lafiya,



Abin ya matukar damun ta y'anda ya mata banza ya daina ko kwanciya a kusa da ita,



Har suka kammala umara suka fara siyayya ita bata iya zuwa ko ina ba su Samra ne dai da Hanan sai gabzar kaya sukeyi kamar hauka mazajen su sun bude musu Aljihun su su fanta ma,




Ta gefen Khalid da kanshi ya ke mata tsaraba batareda sanin ta ba ya kara mata siyayya har ma da su momyn shi da kuma unborn child dinshi,



Sun baro kasa mai tsarki w'anda Haneefa ba ibadar da tayi saboda wannan jarababben baccin baifi sau biyu taje bakin ka'aba ba,



Su Hanan sun koma Nigeria cikin farin ciki su kuma suka koma gidan su a Germany, jitayi kamar ta bisu gida kodan taga su Ummi duk da kusan kullum sai sunyi waya har ma da video call,

Bayan komawar su Germany gaba daya rayuwar zafi tayi mata tarasa farin cikin ta gaba daya saboda sharewar da Khalid yayi mata sai dai kullum ka fin ya fita zai leko dakin ta ya ce bata bukatar wani Abu?


Zata ce ba komai sai ya tafi wurin sana'ar shi idan  tanajin yunwa ma Akwai mai kawo mata koda yaushe,


Idan ya dawo gidan ma baya ko kallon ta zai  yita sha anin shi tana kallon shi duk Abin ya dame ta gashi ta kasa fadawa koda y'an uwan ta ne,


.a kwance take a falo tana chart a wani group din matan Aure dake koyar da mata y'anda zasu tsara rayuwar Auran su cikin jin dadi,



A nan  wani tunani ya fado mata tayi murmushi tareda tashi zaune "a yau sai mun shirya my hubby dole yau zaka daina fushi dani,


Ta shafo cikin ta da yanzu takejin soyayyar shi har cikin bargon ta w'anda a yanzu ya tasarma wata uku,

And he needs her very badly ji yake kamar zai mutu akan son kasan cewa da ita,
Kai dole ma ya nemi shiri domin Haneefa ba irin macen da namiji zai juyawa baya da sunan horo bace sai shi ya horu,

Ta mike tareda kwashe kayan ta nufi kitchen dasu, tana dawowa ta kwanta a bayan shi akan doguwar kujerar da yake zaune a kasan ta,
